Together, these results demonstrate that the geometric relationship between scene depth and ego motion provides a direct way to couple world and action generation within a simple unified model.

</details>


### [104] [CogWAM: Aligning Semantic Cognition with World Action Modeling via Event-Driven Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37721)
*Sen Wang,Liu Liu,Xinjiang Wang,Zequn Chen,Haoyi Jiang,Taojun Ding,Tingyang Xiao,Zhizhong Su,Jie Wang,Sanping Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CogWAM通过持久化的语义状态连接任务推理与世界预测、动作生成，在仿真基准和真实双臂操作中展现出有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义推理与未来世界预测即使同时用于机器人策略，也未必能确保预测和动作与任务进度一致。现有策略需要一种机制，在多个动作片段间保留任务上下文，并将其有效传递至预测与控制。

Method: CogWAM维护语义状态，记录已完成的任务事件和当前子任务，并仅在观测到语义转变时更新。模型通过进度条件化的 WORLD 与 ACTION 查询提取任务相关信息，分别用于未来世界预测和动作生成；训练时两个分支共享语义状态提供的任务进度信息，推理时则移除未来预测分支，依据观测和持续维护的状态直接生成动作。此外，作者提出语义训练策略，以改善转变学习和闭环条件化。

Result: 无需额外的机器人动作预训练，CogWAM在RoboDojo上取得15.56分和11.70%的成功率，在BiCoord上达到当前最佳表现。真实世界实验展示了闭环双臂操作；与逐步更新相比，语义状态重生成次数减少16.4次。

Conclusion: 显式、持久的语义状态可将任务进度信息贯穿于世界预测和动作控制，并减少频繁状态重建，为具备任务级上下文的闭环机器人操作提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Robot policies increasingly incorporate semantic reasoning and future-world prediction, yet combining these capabilities does not guarantee that local predictions and actions remain aligned with task progress. We introduce CogWAM, a cognition-guided world-action model that establishes an explicit semantic interface between task reasoning and world-action learning through a persistent Semantic State, which stores completed task events and the active subtask. CogWAM updates this state only when observations indicate semantic transitions, allowing task-level context to persist across multiple action chunks. To bridge semantic context with physical prediction and control, CogWAM employs progress-conditioned WORLD and ACTION queries that selectively extract task-relevant information for future-world prediction and action generation. During training, the Semantic State provides shared task-progress context for both branches, while inference removes the future-prediction branch and directly generates actions from observations and the maintained state. We further introduce semantic training strategies to improve transition learning and closed-loop conditioning. Without additional robot-action pretraining, CogWAM achieves 15.56 / 11.70 % Score/SR on RoboDojo and state-of-the-art performance on BiCoord, while real-world experiments demonstrate closed-loop dual-arm manipulation with 16.4 fewer Semantic State regenerations than step-wise updating.

</details>


### [105] [When to Adapt: Multi-Signal Domain Shift Detection for Efficient Training-Free Adaptation in Open-Vocabulary Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37602)
*Michele Antonazzi,Alejandra C. Hernandez,José Araujo,Olov Andersson,Patric Jensfelt*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种多信号域偏移检测方法，仅在必要时触发开放词汇分割模型的训练免适应，从而减少长期机器人部署中的适应开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉基础模型虽提升了开放词汇语义分割能力，但环境变化会造成域偏移并降低性能。现有逐帧适应方式计算开销较大，不适合资源受限的机器人硬件。

Method: 在连续帧间利用时间一致性，联合监测视觉变化、适配器失配和语义漂移，以检测域偏移并按需触发训练免持续测试时适应。

Result: 在涵盖室内、室外环境及真实机器人数据的基准上，该方法在维持分割准确率的同时显著减少适应次数。

Conclusion: 多信号触发机制降低了训练免适应的计算负担，使其更适用于长期、真实环境中的机器人部署。

Abstract: Robust and reliable perception is essential for autonomous robots operating in real-world environments, particularly in long-term missions where environmental conditions may change significantly over time. Although recent advances in Visual Foundation Models (VFMs) have improved open-vocabulary semantic segmentation, these models can still suffer from domain shift, which can significantly degrade performance if they are not adapted to the current environment. Training-free domain adaptation is a relevant paradigm for adaptation, consisting of adjusting the model online using lightweight adapters. Recent approaches apply this on a per-frame basis, which is impractical for deployments on resource-constrained robotic hardware. To tackle this, we propose a multi-signal domain shift detection method for training-free continual test-time adaptation (TF-CTTA) in open-vocabulary segmentation. Our method leverages temporal coherence across consecutive frames by monitoring and combining complementary aspects of domain shift (visual change, adapter mismatch, and semantic drift) to trigger adaptation only when needed. We validate our approach on a benchmark including indoor and outdoor environments and using real robotic data. We demonstrate that our approach maintains segmentation accuracy while substantially reducing adaptations, making training-free adaptation practical and feasible for long-term, real-world robotic deployments.

</details>


### [106] [RawVLA: Embodied Neural Image Signal Processor For Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37530)
*Shuhong Liu,Heng Zhou,Lingfeng Qian,Yuhao Fang,Xianbao Hou,Qianyu Zhou,Lin Gu,Wei Sui,Jianfei Yang,Ziteng Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文研究图像信号处理（ISP）对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的影响，并提出自适应 RAW 图像处理方法 RawVLA 及配套基准 RawVLA-Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 通常依赖固定 ISP 输出的 RGB 图像，未将成像流程纳入学习与评估，因而可能忽视相机成像变化对动作预测和操作成功率的影响。

Method: 系统分析增益、传感器噪声、色彩响应、色调响应和位深五项 ISP 因素；提出流式神经 ISP RawVLA，为冻结的 VLA 策略自适应渲染 RAW 观测；构建 RawVLA-Bench，在正常及不利采集条件下评估 RAW 域操作表现。

Result: 实验显示，不同 ISP 因素对动作预测和操作成功率的影响程度不同。RawVLA 在标准成像条件下保持性能，并在成像退化条件下显著提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自适应 RAW 处理可作为物理相机与具身策略之间的有效接口；将成像流程纳入评估，有助于更全面地衡量 VLA 在真实成像变化下的表现。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models typically operate on RGB images produced by a fixed camera image signal processor (ISP), leaving the imaging pipeline outside the learning and evaluation loop. We systematically examine the consequences of this overlooked design choice across five fundamental ISP dimensions: gain, sensor noise, chromatic response, tonal response, and bit depth. Our analysis reveals that RAW-to-RGB processing materially shapes both action prediction and manipulation success, with different ISP dimensions exerting substantially different effects. Guided by these findings, we introduce RawVLA, a streaming neural ISP that adaptively renders RAW observations for frozen VLA policies while concentrating its capacity on the imaging factors relevant to embodied behavior. We further present RawVLA-Bench, a RAW-domain manipulation benchmark to expose image processing as an explicit evaluation variable across clean and adverse acquisition conditions. Experiments on RawVLA-Bench show that RawVLA preserves performance under standard conditions while substantially improving robustness under degraded imaging, establishing adaptive RAW processing as an effective interface between physical cameras and embodied policies.

</details>


### [107] [Learning Social Navigation from Internet Videos in the Policy State Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37476)
*Jiaming Wang,Duc Thang Nguyen,Jizhuo Chen,Volodymyr Shcherbyna,Diwen Liu,Zhengcheng Shen,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种从普通单目步行视频生成社交导航训练环境的高效流程；训练出的策略在仿真基准和真实机器人测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建包含多样场景、地形和行人行为的导航模拟环境成本高昂，限制了鲁棒策略的训练。

Method: 将静态场景表示为度量可通行性地图，并从视频中提取行人轨迹并按时间回放。通过在策略状态空间中定义前向动力学，可模拟反事实机器人状态，无需重建或渲染逼真观测。

Result: 策略在独立 Arena 基准中的成功率为 81.2%，高于最强基线的 75.0%；在真实机器人测试中无需微调即完成 20 次试验中的 19 次。

Conclusion: 该方法可直接利用普通单目视频构建高效训练环境，并支持训练出能够迁移至真实机器人的社交导航策略。

Abstract: Training robust social-navigation policies requires simulators with diverse scene layouts, terrain, and human motion, but constructing such environments and specifying pedestrian behavior is costly. We propose an efficient pipeline that converts ordinary monocular walking videos directly into closed-loop social-navigation training environments in the policy's state space. Our key observation is that local social navigation primarily depends on two types of information: where the robot can traverse and how nearby pedestrians move. We therefore represent the static scene as a metric traversability map, which can be rigidly transformed under counterfactual robot motion, while directly replaying the pedestrian trajectories recovered from the video over time. This abstraction allows us to define the forward dynamics directly in the policy's state space and efficiently simulate counterfactual robot states without reconstructing or rendering photorealistic observations. The resulting policy achieves 81.2% success in the independent Arena benchmark, compared with 75.0% for the strongest baseline, and succeeds in 19/20 real-robot trials without policy fine-tuning.

</details>


### [108] [Encore: Few-Shot Agentic Discovery of Manipulation Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37359)
*Yifan Kang,Zihan Wang,Zhiwen Fan,Bangya Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ENCORE 将少量机器人示范转化为可供编码智能体检查的证据包，使其编写、迭代并冻结任务策略；在多个基准和真实机器人任务上取得了较强表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭自然语言指令，机器人任务往往缺少抓取方式、接触顺序及目标状态等关键信息，智能体不得不通过试错推断。ENCORE旨在利用少量示范补足这些信息，同时避免将示范作为训练数据。

Method: 确定性构建器将每段示范整理为包含多视角关键帧、夹爪事件、帧序列及完整轨迹的证据包。编码智能体据此编写调用固定感知与动作API的策略程序，通过少量开发阶段的 rollout 迭代，并在不提供成功信号的封闭评测前冻结程序。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO上，使用示范时，智能体的首个程序在一半受扰动任务中成功；无示范时则仅在一个任务中成功。冻结后的程序成功率为96.3%，高于使用相同语言模型运行的最强既有智能体系统（89.3%）。在目标未明示的RoboDojo任务上，无示范时没有程序成功。系统还在真实双臂机器人上分别用五段示范完成了方块交接和杯子翻转任务。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结构化示范证据能够帮助编码智能体推断未写明的机器人操作细节，并生成在封闭评测中表现稳健的策略；对于目标信息不足的任务，示范尤为关键。

Abstract: Coding agents can now write, run, and debug programs with little human help. Robot tasks, however, are usually specified by a sentence that leaves out how to grasp, in what order to make contact, and what the result should look like, and an agent given only the sentence must find these details by trial and error. We introduce ENCORE, which gives the agent a few demonstrations as evidence to read rather than as training data. A deterministic builder distills each demonstration into a pack of multi-view keyframes, gripper events, frame strips, and the full trajectory. A coding agent studies the pack, writes a policy program against a fixed perception and action API, refines it iteratively over a few development rollouts, and freezes it before a sealed evaluation that never reveals the success signal. On LIBERO-PRO, the agent's first program already succeeds in half of the perturbed tasks with demonstrations and in one task without them, and the frozen programs outperform the strongest prior agentic system run with the same language model (96.3% against 89.3%). On RoboDojo tasks whose instructions leave the goal unstated, no program succeeds without demonstrations. ENCORE also runs on a real bimanual robot, learning cube handover and cup inversion from five demonstrations each.

</details>


### [109] [V2X-WAM: A Cooperative World Action Model for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37098)
*Junwei You,Weizhe Tang,Can Wang,Yan Zhao,Jun Hua,Haotian Shi,Wei Zhang,Lin Wang,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出V2X-WAM，一种面向车路协同自动驾驶的世界动作模型，通过联合场景理解、动作生成与未来世界推理，使规划器能够显式预测驾驶决策对交通环境演化的影响，从而提升规划精度、安全性与通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车路协同驾驶方法主要利用路侧信息增强当前场景表示，较少显式建模候选驾驶动作可能引发的未来交通变化，导致规划器难以预测自身决策与动态环境之间的交互影响。同时，基础设施信息传输还面临通信开销较大的问题。

Method: V2X-WAM首先融合车辆端与基础设施端观测，构建具备可靠性感知能力的时空表示，并将基础设施信息压缩为紧凑的量化消息以降低通信成本。在此基础上，多模态规划器生成候选未来轨迹，并以轨迹为条件预测未来占用状态和动态交通流；随后将预测到的未来世界后果反馈给规划模块，迭代修正轨迹，形成动作生成与未来环境演化之间的闭环交互。

Result: 在大规模真实世界车路协同驾驶数据集上的实验表明，V2X-WAM相较代表性端到端协同驾驶方法，在规划准确性和安全性方面均取得稳定提升；同时，其未来世界预测能力更强，通信开销显著降低。消融实验进一步验证了可靠性感知表示、信息量化、未来世界预测及反馈式规划等设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将车路协同感知、动作规划与未来世界建模紧密结合，能够增强自动驾驶系统对决策后果的预见能力。V2X-WAM在保持较低通信负担的同时，实现了更准确、更安全的协同规划，为面向交互式未来预测的车路协同自动驾驶提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vehicle-infrastructure cooperation can complement onboard sensing with broader and more informative observations of the traffic environment, providing valuable support for end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing cooperative driving methods mainly exploit roadside information to enhance the representation of the current scene, while the future consequences of prospective driving actions are rarely modeled explicitly. This limits the ability of the planner to anticipate how its decisions may interact with the evolving traffic environment. To address this issue, we propose V2X-WAM, a cooperative world action model that tightly couples cooperative scene understanding, action generation, and future-world reasoning. V2X-WAM constructs a reliability-aware spatiotemporal representation from vehicle- and infrastructure-side observations, while compressing infrastructure information into a compact quantized message for efficient communication. Based on the resulting cooperative representation, a multimodal planner generates prospective trajectories, which explicitly condition future occupancy and dynamic-flow prediction. The predicted world consequences are then fed back to refine the planned trajectory, forming a closed interaction between action and future-world evolution. Experiments on a large-scale real-world cooperative driving dataset demonstrate that V2X-WAM consistently improves planning accuracy and safety over representative end-to-end cooperative driving methods, while achieving stronger future-world prediction and substantially lower communication overhead. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed design.

</details>


### [110] [Track-and-Complete: Learning Humanoid Skills from a Single Failed Human Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36924)
*Sarmad Idrees,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACC利用失败的人类演示视频训练人形机器人：先模仿失败前可用的动作片段，再依据推断出的任务目标完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习人形机器人技能通常依赖成功的人类演示，而获取此类演示可能需要专门采集数据。失败视频虽未完成任务，仍可能包含有用的动作前缀及预期目标。

Method: TRACC先将失败发生前的有效轨迹片段作为先验知识；当动作到达失败点后，再利用任务完成奖励引导策略学习目标，无需成功轨迹示范。

Result: 作者在Oops!数据集中的六项真实场景失败任务上进行了评估，实验结果显示，该方法在没有成功示范时仍能从失败尝试中学习。

Conclusion: 失败的人类视频可作为人形机器人技能学习的有效监督来源。

Abstract: Learning humanoid skills from videos typically requires a successful human demonstration, which often demands custom data collection. Although failures have traditionally been treated only as negative examples in robot learning, they can still reveal a usable trajectory prefix before the task fails, as well as the intended outcome. To leverage this information from a failed-attempt video, we propose TRACC, a pipeline that imitates the useful portion of the motion trajectory and then completes the task based on the inferred task outcome. The usable motion prefix serves as prior knowledge until the failure occurs, after which the task-completion reward guides the policy to learn the intended task goal without requiring a successful task trajectory. We evaluate our method on six in-the-wild failed human tasks from the Oops! dataset. Our experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach for learning from failed attempts when no successful demonstration is available. Thus, these findings establish failed human videos as a viable source of supervision for humanoid skill learning.

</details>


### [111] [DRHeC: Differentiable Rendering for Hand-Eye Calibration with RGB-Based Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36779)
*Xiaotian Zhang,Yusheng Wang,Naoya Kagawa,Noritaka Takamura,Keiji Okuhara,Hiroyasu Baba,Jun Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于 RGB 可微渲染的无标记手眼标定方法，通过融合颜色与掩码几何特征提升精度和优化稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统标记法依赖标记精度与可观测性；现有可微渲染方法使用二值掩码，可能丢失物体内部轮廓细节，并出现优化不稳定和局部最优。

Method: 构建融合 RGB 信息与掩码几何特征的可微渲染框架，以提供更丰富的外观和几何线索；同时提出掩码引导的图像到图像翻译方法，显式保持颜色与几何一致性。

Result: 仿真和真实实验表明，该方法具有较高精度与鲁棒性。在 UR5e 实验中，抓取成功率为 88.9%，插入成功率为 57.4%，分别比 EasyHeC 高 46.3 和 48.1 个百分点。

Conclusion: 该 RGB 可微渲染方案改善了无标记手眼标定的精度与优化稳定性，并在下游抓取和插入任务中优于现有可微渲染基线。

Abstract: Accurate hand-eye calibration is crucial for precision manipulation. Traditional methods rely on markers, with their precision dependent on marker accuracy and observability. In contrast, markerless methods, such as learning-based approaches, use deep neural networks to directly extract keypoints or features from images, enabling the computation of hand-eye transformation with a single image and without the need for physical markers. Recently, differentiable rendering-based methods for hand-eye calibration have leveraged physical models to render binary masks and compare them with observations, enabling hand-eye calibration without fiducial markers in the calibration stage and providing interpretable optimization. While the state-of-the-art differentiable rendering methods achieve remarkable accuracy, the use of binary masks can result in the loss of internal profile details, reducing precision. Additionally, these methods can also suffer from unstable optimization and local minima. In this study, we propose a novel RGB-based differentiable rendering framework that provides richer geometric and appearance cues by incorporating color and mask geometric features, thereby improving calibration accuracy and optimization stability. Additionally, we propose a mask-guided image-to-image translation method to ensure explicit preservation of color and geometric consistency throughout the translation. Our approach is validated through both simulation and real-world experiments, with results demonstrating strong accuracy and robustness and clear improvements over existing differentiable rendering methods. Our method achieves a grasping success rate of 88.9% and insertion success rate of 57.4% on the UR5e real-world experiment, outperforming the state-of-the-art differentiable rendering hand-eye calibration method EasyHeC by 46.3 and 48.1 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [112] [Distilling Privileged Control Barrier Functions into RGB-Only Safety Filters for Dynamic Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36520)
*Seungyeon Yoo,Gawon Lee,Seungwoo Jung,Inkyu Jang,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向动态环境的RGB-only视觉安全过滤器，通过教师—学生视觉蒸馏，将特权信息下的控制屏障函数（CBF）安全行为迁移至仅依赖RGB历史和机器人速度的学生策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端RGB导航策略在真实动态环境中容易发生碰撞。现有视觉CBF方法通常依赖实时渲染或显式三维场景重建，且主要针对静态场景，不利于机载实时部署，因此需要一种无需在线重建、能够处理动态障碍物的轻量安全层。

Method: 构建教师—学生视觉蒸馏框架。教师在真实到仿真的动态Gaussian Splatting环境中利用真实机器人状态和障碍物状态生成CBF安全约束；为缩小信息差，教师仅使用学生RGB历史中可观测的障碍物生成约束。教师还建模障碍物速度不确定性，以增强对运动变化的鲁棒性。学生以短时RGB历史、机器人速度和名义控制动作为输入，直接输出安全动作；动作增强则提供多样的安全与不安全名义动作，帮助学生学习安全边界。

Result: 实验表明，该方法优于视觉CBF基线，并能在动态障碍物运动条件下提升基于RGB的导航策略安全性。部署时无需显式三维重建或在线渲染，仅需RGB观测和机器人速度。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，基于教师—学生蒸馏的视觉安全过滤器能够有效将特权状态下的CBF安全行为迁移到RGB-only系统，在保持较低部署开销的同时提升动态环境中的导航安全性与实用性。

Abstract: RGB-only end-to-end visual navigation policies remain vulnerable to collisions in real-world dynamic environments, motivating a dedicated safety layer. Existing visual Control Barrier Function (CBF) approaches seek to provide safety from RGB observations, but often rely on real-time rendering or explicit scene reconstruction and are primarily designed for static scenes, limiting their practicality for onboard deployment. We propose a teacher-student visual distillation framework that transfers the safety behavior of a privileged CBF teacher to an RGB-only student filter for dynamic environments. The student maps a short RGB history, robot velocity, and a nominal control action directly to a safe action, while the teacher uses ground-truth robot and obstacle states in a real-to-sim dynamic Gaussian Splatting environment. To reduce the teacher-student information gap, the teacher constructs safety constraints only from obstacles observable within the student's RGB history. It also accounts for obstacle-velocity uncertainty to improve robustness to motion variations, while action augmentation exposes the student to diverse safe and unsafe nominal actions to better capture the safety boundary. At deployment, the student requires only RGB observations and robot velocity, without explicit 3D reconstruction or online rendering. Experiments show that the proposed method outperforms visual CBF baselines and improves the safety of RGB-based navigation policies under dynamic obstacle motion. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [113] [Staircase Policy: Streaming Inference for World-Action Models with Large Action Chunks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36471)
*Guoheng Sun,Chen Chen,Jin Wang,Ang Li,Teresa Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: STAIRCASE POLICY 将流式推理与分阶段动作分块结合，使世界动作模型能够在持续执行中更新未来预测和未执行动作，从而兼顾长时域表现与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型通过预测未来观测来辅助动作生成，但未来预测会增加迭代推理开销。大动作块虽可摊销成本，却因后续动作依赖过时观测而导致长时域性能下降。

Method: 将流匹配视觉-语言-动作策略转化为 JEPA 风格的世界动作模型，并在不同去噪阶段错开生成和执行动作子块。近端动作可先行执行，后续动作继续细化；到达子块边界时，根据最新观测重新预测未来潜变量，并据此更新所有尚未执行的动作。未来预测误差还可用于自适应调整分块。

Result: S-WAM 在 LIBERO 和 LIBERO-Plus 上分别达到 97.7% 和 87.9%，并在多种策略骨干及真实机器人任务上提升表现。其执行吞吐量为每秒 292.7 个动作，在准确率相当时达到传统执行方式的 3.62 倍；首次动作延迟从 123.6 毫秒降至 73.3 毫秒。结合额外推理优化后，吞吐量可达每秒 642.9 个动作。

Conclusion: 通过在动作执行过程中持续刷新未来预测并更新待执行动作，该框架缓解了长动作块依赖陈旧观测的问题，同时降低响应延迟、提高吞吐量。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) improve robotic manipulation by conditioning action generation on predicted future observations, but future prediction adds further inference overhead to already expensive iterative action generation. Action chunking can amortize this cost over multiple actions, yet performance degrades over long execution horizons because later actions remain conditioned on stale observations. We introduce STAIRCASE POLICY, a streaming inference and training framework that turns a flow-matching VLA into a JEPA-style WAM and partitions a large action chunk into sub-chunks at staggered denoising stages. Near-term actions are executed as soon as they become available, while later actions continue to be refined. At each sub-chunk boundary, the future latent is re-predicted from the latest observation and used to update all unexecuted actions, enabling long-horizon execution without repeated full policy inference. The resulting future-prediction error can further serve as a signal for adaptive chunking. S-WAM achieves 97.7% on LIBERO and 87.9% on LIBERO-Plus, and improves performance across multiple policy backbones and real-robot tasks. It reaches 292.7 executed actions per second, $3.62\times$ the throughput of conventional execution at comparable accuracy, while reducing time-to-first-action from 123.6 to 73.3 ms. With additional inference optimizations, throughput further increases to 642.9 actions per second.

</details>


### [114] [FineART: Fine-grained Annotated Robotic Trajectory Dataset and Vision-Language-Action Model for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36416)
*Jade Choghari,Pepijn Kooijmans,Mansi Agarwal,Yusuf Umut Ciftci,Aseem Doriwala,Catherine Weaver,Mouli Sivapurapu,Kai Yang,Jackson Lee,Thomas Wolf,Pragna Mannam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FineART 是面向双臂机器人长时程操作的密集标注数据集；配套的 FineART-VLA 通过预测下一子任务进行中期训练，提升任务成功率、数据效率和跨任务泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有操作数据集虽包含大量单臂轨迹，但通常每个 episode 只有一条高层指令；双臂数据集的子任务标注也覆盖有限，难以支持复杂、多步骤的长时程操作学习。

Method: 构建 FineART，收录 40,543 个 episode、1,718 小时数据、533,913 个子任务，覆盖 151 项任务；提出 FineART-VLA，使策略能够预测自身的下一子任务，并评估子任务预测式中期训练及逐步人工子任务指导的效果。

Result: 空间消歧任务成功率由 32.0% 提高至 100.0%；在未见过的长时程任务中，逐步人工子任务指导使成功率由 16.0% 提高至 76.0%。在新机器人上进行少量微调后，所需数据量仅为未采用中期训练基线的十分之一，并能零样本泛化至新硬件上的完全未见任务。

Conclusion: 密集的双臂子任务标注与子任务预测式中期训练，有助于提升机器人长时程操作的表现、跨硬件数据效率及任务泛化能力。作者开放了数据集、模型权重和训练代码。

Abstract: Robots operating in real-world environments must execute complex, multi-step bimanual tasks over long horizons rather than single, isolated actions. Current manipulation datasets struggle to support this capability: although single-arm datasets reach hundreds of thousands of trajectories, they typically provide only one high-level instruction per episode while the rare bimanual effort that does label subtasks annotates only a fraction of its hours. We present FineART, a densely annotated bimanual manipulation dataset of 40,543 episodes, 1,718 hours, and 533,913 subtasks across 151 tasks. We also introduce FineART-VLA, a vision-language-action policy that predicts its own next subtask, and show that mid-training it this way yields substantial gains. Specifically, success on a spatial disambiguation task increases from 32.0% to 100.0%, and step-by-step human subtask guidance lifts success on an unseen long-horizon task from 16.0% to 76.0%. Furthermore, after minimal fine-tuning on a new robot, the policy requires one-tenth the data of baselines without mid-training and generalizes zero-shot to completely unseen tasks on the new hardware. We open-source the full dataset, model weights, and training code.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Direct Experience World-Model Optimization: Learning the World Beyond Action Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37398)
*Xiangcheng Zhan,Zirui Chen,Yicheng Zhao,Ziteng Gao,Shuo Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DEWO利用部署过程中获得的视觉经验持续优化世界模型，并结合动作模仿与推理时引导，提升世界—动作模型的操控表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部署后学习通常只改善行为，不要求世界预测同步变准。灵巧操控中的微小误差容易在高维动作空间中累积，使策略难以改进，并可能让交互超出世界模型的训练分布。

Method: DEWO识别交互转折点，从成功与失败的后续视觉观测中学习世界表征，并以此支持无分类器引导；同时训练价值头，根据视频表征估计任务进度，在推理时于进度停滞时启动引导。该方法将视觉监督与动作模仿结合，用于部署后优化。

Result: 在五项 DexJoCo 任务中，DEWO提升了三种世界—动作模型的平均成功率。消融实验表明，相较于仅使用动作监督，成功与失败后续过程的视觉监督能同时改善预测和控制。在 Wuji 与 Sharpa 平台的四项真实任务中，经过两轮部署学习，在初始至少成功一次的评估单元中，成功率由51.0%升至71.7%，提高20.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 部署经验可用于持续优化世界预测，并通过更准确的世界表征改善动作生成与控制。研究结果支持将预测学习纳入世界—动作模型的部署适应过程。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) couple action generation with predictions of how physical interactions unfold. However, current post-deployment learning paradigms typically improve behavior without requiring better world predictions. Especially in dexterous manipulation, small execution errors can compound in high-dimensional action spaces, hindering policy improvement and pushing interactions beyond the world model's training distribution. Motivated by this, we propose Direct Experience World-Model Optimization (DEWO), a post-deployment learning paradigm for WAMs that, alongside action imitation, refines world representations through visual experience to better condition action generation. Specifically, it identifies interaction turning points and learns from successful and failed futures to support classifier-free guidance. An additional value head estimates task progress from video representations and activates guidance when progress stalls during inference. Across five DexJoCo tasks, DEWO improves average success across all three WAM formulations. Ablations show that visual supervision from successful and failed continuations improves both prediction and control beyond action supervision alone. On four real-world tasks across Wuji and Sharpa, 3 x 3 grid evaluations show that two rounds of deployment learning increase success from 51.0% to 71.7% in cells with at least one initial success, a gain of 20.7 percentage points. These findings support continued predictive learning for improving control through deployment experience, making world modeling an active part of WAM adaptation.

</details>


### [116] [Stochastic World Models for Verifying Vision-Based Neural Feedback Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38120)
*I. Samuel Akinwande,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Clark Barrett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向视觉神经反馈系统验证的随机世界模型，并结合多种形式化分析技术提升闭环系统的可验证性。该模型在保持可验证性的同时，比GAN感知代理更准确地重现观测，并显著扩大可解析状态空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉反馈控制器的验证需要一个能够准确描述传感器观测变化的代理模型，但现有GAN代理通常参数规模大、复杂场景重现能力有限且难以形式化验证。因此，研究旨在构建更具表现力、同时适合验证的感知代理，并解决验证过程中状态空间难以完全覆盖的问题。

Method: 构建带有物理意义潜变量的随机世界模型，其运算由现有验证器可处理的操作组成。验证流程融合反例搜索、自适应细化、符号分析和反向分析，以逐步缩小不确定区域并判断系统行为。研究在紧急制动基准上分别使用GAN代理和RGB世界模型代理进行评估。

Result: 世界模型在参数量最多少于GAN代理130倍的情况下，对留出视频帧的重现更加准确。在采用GAN代理的基准实验中，所提出方法解析了完整状态空间，而先进验证器仍有38%的状态空间未解析。在此前缺乏验证结果的RGB基准上，世界模型代理使超过80%的状态空间得到解析。

Conclusion: 随机世界模型能够在感知保真度与形式化可验证性之间取得更好的平衡。结合反例驱动和符号化的混合验证流程，可显著提高视觉神经反馈系统的状态空间覆盖率，说明该方法适合作为复杂视觉闭环系统的可验证感知代理。

Abstract: Verifying a vision-based neural feedback system requires a model of the observations its controller acts upon. Such a model must capture the variation the sensor produces, while remaining tractable for closed-loop analysis. Generative adversarial networks (GANs) have served as perception surrogates, but they are large, reproduce complex scenes poorly, and are hard to verify. We explore stochastic world models as a richer class of perception surrogates. We train a world model with physically grounded latents, built from operations that standard verifiers bound. It reproduces held-out frames more faithfully than GAN surrogates with up to 130 times as many parameters. To verify these surrogates, we develop a procedure that combines falsification, adaptive refinement, symbolic, and backward analyses. On an emergency braking benchmark with a GAN surrogate, our procedure resolves the entire state space, 38% of which the state-of-the-art verifier left unresolved. On the RGB version of the benchmark, where no verification results have previously been reported, our procedure resolves over 80% of the state space with a world model surrogate.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond a single latent space: a dual-latent world model for long-horizon planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37644)
*Delin Zhao,Zhengrong Yue,Shaobin Zhuang,Junlin He,Xiaoyu Chen,Zikang Wang,Yuxin Liu,Limin Wang,Yali Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Dual-WM通过区分短期执行与长期规划的潜在状态和动力学模型，并采用多时间尺度加权训练，提升了长时域目标条件视觉控制表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型虽然能准确预测短期状态，但递归滚动会累积误差；高维潜在空间中的距离集中也会削弱目标辨别能力，限制长时域规划。

Method: Dual-WM由低层动作条件模型和高层宏动作规划模型构成：高层生成潜在子目标，低层将其细化为可执行动作。LoRe在两层监督模型自生成的预测，并依据递归误差传播分析，为不同时间尺度设置不同衰减率的指数加权。

Result: 在五项从零训练的目标条件视觉控制任务中，目标间隔为50步时，平均成功率由75.9%升至84.4%；间隔为100步时，由61.4%升至69.5%。在100步间隔下，Dual-WM在全部五项任务中优于相应最强基线，且平均成功率比LeWM高30.8个百分点。消融和分析结果还显示，其目标评估表征更具信息量，递归预测一致性更高。

Conclusion: 将短期执行与长期规划分工，并针对多个时间尺度训练潜在表示，有助于缓解递归误差和目标辨别不足，从而提升潜在模型的长时域规划可靠性。

Abstract: Latent world models often struggle with long-horizon planning despite accurate short-term predictions. Recursive rollouts accumulate errors, while distance concentration in high-dimensional latent spaces can weaken goal discrimination. We introduce the Dual-Latent World Model (Dual-WM), which separates local execution and long-range planning through distinct state representations and dynamics models. The low-level model predicts action-conditioned transitions, while the high-level model uses learned macro-actions to plan over longer temporal spans. We also propose Long-Horizon Representation Learning with Weighted Rollout (LoRe), which supervises self-generated predictions at both levels. An analysis of recursive error propagation motivates exponential horizon weights with separate decay rates for the two temporal scales. During planning, the high-level model generates latent subgoals that the low-level model refines into actions for precise execution. We evaluate from-scratch Dual-WM on five goal-conditioned visual control tasks against the task-wise strongest baselines without actor-guided proposals. At goal offsets of 50 and 100 environment steps, mean success increases from 75.9% to 84.4% and from 61.4% to 69.5%, respectively. At offset 100, Dual-WM outperforms these baselines on all five tasks and improves mean success over LeWM by 30.8 percentage points. Ablations and supporting analyses provide evidence of more informative representations for goal evaluation and greater consistency under recursive prediction. These results highlight the value of separating temporal roles and training across multiple horizons for reliable latent planning. Our core implementation is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [118] [Seek Before You Move: Evidence Seeking for Progress Grounding in Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37353)
*Zhimin Wang,Meiyuan Zhu,Duo Wu,Linjia Kang,Yajun Wang,Yuan Ni,Xiaohang Wang,Tianlu Pan,Jingyan Jiang,Yaowei Wang,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SeekVLN，通过主动获取任务相关视觉证据，缓解视觉语言导航中的“进度近视”问题，并在模拟基准上取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型的导航智能体通常依赖当前可见信息推理；当关键地标不在视野中时，仍可能对进度判断过度自信，因证据不足而走错方向。

Method: SeekVLN 分两阶段训练。第一阶段，未来引导的反向生成（FRG）利用未来专家动作扩充离线轨迹，加入补充视角和证据标注，并通过监督微调建立主动寻证与进度推理能力。第二阶段，反事实对比策略优化（C2PO）将寻证分支与同一状态下直接导航的反事实分支进行比较，根据后续导航收益通过对比奖励评估寻证决策。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 和 RxR-CE 模拟基准上，SeekVLN 相较基础模型的成功率分别提升 12.7% 和 7.5%，达到当前最佳表现。模拟与真实环境评估还显示，该方法能表现出类似人类的主动寻证行为。

Conclusion: 主动获取任务相关证据，并根据其对后续导航的实际收益进行优化，有助于提升进度判断的可靠性和长程导航表现。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to continuously ground task progress from long-horizon instructions and partial egocentric observations. Existing VLM-based navigation agents typically reason only over available observations and may remain confident even when task-relevant evidence is missing. For example, an agent may confidently proceed forward and get lost even though the landmark indicating the next turn lies outside its current field of view. We term this failure mode Progress Myopia: the agent fails to recognize unreliable progress grounding and continues acting on insufficient evidence. To address it, we propose SeekVLN, an evidence-seeking framework that couples semantic progress reasoning with active acquisition of task-relevant observations. SeekVLN is trained in two stages: First, Future-guided Reverse Generation (FRG) uses future expert actions to augment offline expert trajectories with supplementary views and evidence annotations. Supervised fine-tuning on these trajectories establishes a prior for evidence seeking and progress reasoning without additional expert interaction. However, imitation alone does not reveal whether seeking improves subsequent navigation. We therefore introduce Counterfactual Contrastive Policy Optimization (C2PO) for reinforcement fine-tuning. By comparing each evidence-seeking branch with a counterfactual direct-navigation branch from the same state, C2PO uses a contrastive reward to assign credit to seeking decisions based on subsequent navigation benefit. Experiments on simulated benchmarks show that SeekVLN achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving success rate by 12.7% and 7.5% over the base model on R2R-CE and RxR-CE, respectively. Both simulated and real-world evaluations exhibit human-like evidence-seeking behaviors for more reliable progress grounding.

</details>


### [119] [Learning from Viable Failure Prefixes: Milestone Viability Potential Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37111)
*Qi Zhou,Yuanfan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MVPO利用失败轨迹中的可行前缀，为长时程LLM智能体提供更有效的中间决策信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO、GiGPO等组内比较方法在回报缺乏差异时会出现“零信用失败”：早期训练中的失败轨迹即使包含有用前缀，也无法获得区分任务表现的优势信号。

Method: 提出里程碑可行性势能策略优化（MVPO）：基于并查集构建可行区域并估计前缀势能；在组内信用信号为零时，以势能差修正优势；再依据相对性能进展逐步降低势能分支的影响。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct上的实验显示，MVPO优于包括GRPO和GiGPO在内的八种基线。在相同训练时长下，相比GiGPO，ALFWorld成功率提高4.4个百分点，WebShop提高5.3个百分点；优势构造开销仅增加0.16%–0.20%。

Conclusion: MVPO通过从可行的失败前缀中提取学习信号，缓解了稀疏延迟奖励下的零信用问题，并以较低额外开销提升了长时程任务表现。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents require reinforcement learning methods that can assign credit to intermediate decisions under sparse and delayed rewards. Existing group-based methods such as GRPO and GiGPO alleviate this issue by comparing rollout returns or repeated anchor states, but they still fail when the compared returns have no variation. We identify this failure mode as zero-credit failure: during early training, many failed rollouts contain useful prefixes, yet existing methods assign them no task-discriminative advantage. To address this issue, we propose Milestone Viability Potential Policy Optimization (MVPO), a potential-routed policy optimization algorithm that learns from viable failure prefixes. MVPO estimates prefix potential over Union-Find viability regions, repairs zero-credit groups with potential-difference advantages, and attenuates the potential branch according to relative performance progress. Experiments with Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct show that MVPO outperforms eight strong baselines, including GRPO and GiGPO. Under the same training length, MVPO improves over the GiGPO baseline by +4.4 success points on ALFWorld and +5.3 on WebShop, while adding only 0.16%-0.20% advantage-construction overhead.

</details>


### [120] [HorizonFlow: Variable-Length Planning for Offline Goal-Conditioned RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36896)
*JunHyeok Oh,Zian Jang,Byung-Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HorizonFlow 将规划长度作为生成结果，在离线目标条件强化学习中联合生成计划内容与长度，并在多个导航和视觉操作基准上取得最佳平均表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹修复方法通常预先设定规划时域，但合适的时域取决于具体路线：过短可能导致不可行的状态转换，过长则可能产生冗余动作。

Method: 提出分层规划器 HorizonFlow，由子目标路线规划器和动作前缀控制器组成。两者结合基于插入的生成与流匹配，根据已生成的部分计划引导后续插入，并联合生成连续计划内容和计划长度。模型还利用生成的长度信息筛选候选方案、引导生成更短计划，无需单独训练价值模型。

Result: 在 Maze2D、Multi2D 和 OGBench 的导航及视觉操作基准上，HorizonFlow 在所比较方法中取得最高的平均性能。

Conclusion: 将计划长度纳入生成过程，可使规划时域适应路线需求；实验结果表明，HorizonFlow 在多个基准上具有较强表现。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative planning have made trajectory inpainting a promising approach to offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning. However, these methods typically specify the planning horizon before generating plan content, even though the appropriate horizon depends on the route itself. A horizon that is too short can force infeasible transitions, whereas one that is too long can introduce redundant motion. We introduce HorizonFlow, a hierarchical planner that treats plan length as an output of generation rather than a prescribed input. Its subgoal route planner guides its action-prefix controller through a sequence of latent subgoals. Both components combine insertion-based generation with flow matching to jointly generate continuous plan content and length, using the partially generated plan to guide token insertion. HorizonFlow reuses the resulting length information to select candidates and steer generation toward shorter plans without a separate learned value model. Across Maze2D, Multi2D, and OGBench navigation and visual manipulation benchmarks, HorizonFlow achieves the highest average performance among the compared methods.

</details>


### [121] [State Trace Rationale As Auxiliary Task in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36867)
*Muhammad U. Nasir,Alex Vogt,Steven D. James,Julian Togelius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STRAT辅助任务，让深度强化学习智能体在线预测自身状态的简短文本轨迹，从而提升稀疏奖励复杂任务中的学习效果与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习在稀疏奖励和复杂环境中容易学习失败，且内部状态表示可能出现秩坍塌，难以反映智能体对位置、物品、目标和进度的理解。

Method: 在标准策略网络上增加一个辅助预测头，由环境规则在线生成文本监督信号，无需人工标注。文本轨迹融合地标知识、路线知识和全局测量知识，描述智能体的位置、库存、目标及即时进展。

Result: 在60个稀疏奖励的XLand-MiniGrid任务中，STRAT能够解决标准强化学习完全无法解决的复杂环境，同时压缩状态表示并抑制秩坍塌。其预测文本还可在每个时间步提供可读的智能体信念解释，且不产生额外推理成本。

Conclusion: STRAT通过将结构化文本状态预测作为辅助学习目标，有效增强了强化学习的任务解决能力、表示质量和可解释性，是一种无需人工标注且易于集成的改进方案。

Abstract: We propose STRAT, an auxiliary task that trains deep reinforcement learning (RL) agents to predict a short textual trace of their own state. Inspired by human spatial navigation, the description combines landmark, route, and survey knowledge, tracking the agent's position, inventory, goals, and immediate progress. Environment rules generate this text online without human labelling. Our method adds a single auxiliary head to a standard policy. Across 60 sparse-reward XLand-MiniGrid tasks, STRAT solves complex environments where standard RL fails outright, while compacting state representations and preventing rank collapse. Beyond performance gains, the predicted trace provides a readable account of agent beliefs at every step for no extra cost.

</details>


### [122] [Distinguish or Homogenize: Last-Chance Policy Identification and Risk-Budgeted Recovery under Irreversible Resource Depletion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36741)
*Yibo Guo,Xiaodan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“区分或同质化”原则与 Last-Chance Policy Identification（LCPI）框架：智能体不仅可以消耗资源区分潜在故障模型，也可以主动改变系统状态，使不同模型共享可接受的后续策略，从而无需继续诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有识别、规划和诊断方法通常将“模型到可接受策略的映射”视为预先给定，未刻画智能体行动如何改变这一映射。本文旨在研究不可逆资源消耗下，何时应继续诊断，何时应通过改变状态实现模型同质化。

Method: LCPI在智能体到达的状态而非初始状态上评价决策正确性，并提出 Last Identifiable Margin（LIM）作为区分与同质化之间的可行性边界。针对确定性诊断图，给出 Exact-LIM 递归；针对带噪声的有限时域恢复问题，提出 Risk-Budgeted Compatibility Planning（RBCP），在硬性最坏情况失败约束下搜索兼容性前沿。此外，通过成本匹配但保持模型不兼容的假控制实验，检验性能提升来源。

Result: 在抽象微服务拓扑中的事件恢复和 MiniGrid 潜在损伤导航任务中，RBCP在满足失败预算的同时提升了风险可行的恢复表现。假控制完全消除了该性能增益，表明收益并非来自额外搜索或资源，而是来自改变不同模型对应的可接受策略集合。

Conclusion: LCPI将诊断与规划统一为一种面向状态转换的决策问题：智能体可以通过行动降低模型间的策略差异，从而替代部分信息获取。LIM提供了区分与同质化的理论边界，RBCP则展示了该原则在风险受限恢复任务中的实际有效性。

Abstract: Under irreversible resource depletion, an agent can spend resources to distinguish among latent fault models, or to change the system state so that the remaining models admit a common acceptable continuation--at which point further diagnosis becomes unnecessary. This distinguish-or-homogenize principle identifies a path that existing frameworks for identification, planning, and diagnosis do not make explicit: prior formulations treat the mapping from fault models to acceptable policies as a given, whereas LCPI makes it a function of the agent's own actions. We formalize this principle through Last-Chance Policy Identification (LCPI), where correctness is evaluated at the state the agent reaches rather than at the initial state. The Last Identifiable Margin (LIM) marks the feasibility boundary between distinguishing and homogenizing. For deterministic diagnostic graphs we provide the Exact-LIM recursion; for noisy finite-horizon recovery we propose Risk-Budgeted Compatibility Planning (RBCP), which searches a compatibility-aware frontier under a hard worst-case failure constraint. Across incident recovery on abstract microservice topologies and latent-damage navigation in MiniGrid, RBCP improves risk-feasible recovery while satisfying the failure budget. A sham control--cost-matched actions that preserve model incompatibility--eliminates the gain entirely, confirming that the benefit comes from changing which policies are acceptable for which models, not from extra search or additional budget.

</details>


### [123] [The Layer Mystery of VLA: An Information-Theoretical Analysis of VLA Latent Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36118)
*Yuxiang Liu,Lizhi Yang,Fengze Xie,Aaron Ames,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，VLA策略中融合多个视觉语言骨干层通常不如选择单层；以动作条件InfoNCE评分选层，可大幅降低计算开销并接近事后优化的固定层策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在接口应连接骨干网络的哪些层尚不明确。不同骨干与基准上的最佳层差异显著，而逐一训练策略进行穷举评估成本高昂，因此需要有效的层选择依据。

Method: 在三个预训练模型、LIBERO与CALVIN两个操作基准上，使用冻结骨干比较单层选择与多层融合，并为每种配置运行三个训练随机种子。评估三种融合机制、三种层子集策略及四种层质量代理指标；同时推导动作条件InfoNCE与动作预测信息瓶颈目标之间的重加权等价关系。

Result: 54种融合配置中有47种不及表现最佳的单层策略。InfoNCE与策略成功率的正相关最稳定。按InfoNCE选层所需GPU计算量比穷举策略评估少9至33倍；六种设置中的平均选择遗憾为3.71个百分点，优于选择最深层的17.89个百分点，并接近利用全部六次穷举结果事后优化固定层启发式所达到的3.28至3.50个百分点。

Conclusion: 多层融合并非普遍有效，且最佳层具有模型与任务依赖性。动作条件InfoNCE可作为实用的选层代理，在无需闭环策略评估的情况下，以较低成本获得接近强基线的选层效果。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies connect a pretrained vision-language backbone to an action head through a latent interface, but which backbone layers this interface should expose remains unclear. We study single-layer selection and multi-layer fusion for frozen backbones across three pretrained models and two manipulation benchmarks, LIBERO and CALVIN, with three policy-training seeds per configuration. Across three fusion mechanisms and three layer-subset strategies, 47 of 54 configurations underperform the best observed single-layer policy. Our stastical analysis further confirms that fusion's advantage is very limited. However, the best layer varies substantially across backbones and benchmarks, making layer selection consequential and exhaustive policy sweeps expensive. We further derive a reweighting equivalence between the proposed information-bottleneck objectives for action-conditioned InfoNCE and action prediction, motivating InfoNCE as a proxy for layer quality. Empirically, InfoNCE provides the most consistent positive association with policy success among four evaluated proxies. Selecting the layer with the highest InfoNCE score requires 9-33 times less GPU compute than exhaustive policy sweeps and reduces mean selection regret from 17.89 percentage points for deepest-layer selection to 3.71 points across six settings. Its mean regret is close to the 3.28-3.50 points achieved by fixed-layer heuristics optimized retrospectively using all six oracle sweeps, without requiring closed-loop evaluations during selection.

</details>


### [124] [ChronoSRL: Temporal Geometry for Self-Supervised Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36238)
*Nico Bohlinger,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChronoSRL为强化学习中的状态—目标表示引入时间几何，使嵌入距离对应到达目标所需时间，并结合生存式预测提升到达速度、可靠性与停留能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比式和生存式强化学习方法未将表示空间中的距离明确校准为时间；而一次快速到达并不代表策略能稳定到达或持续停留在目标附近。

Method: 训练状态—动作与目标的嵌入距离匹配实际目标到达时间；对未到达的目标及来自其他轨迹的目标，施加至少一个折扣时域的距离约束。基于这些时间嵌入，预测目标到达时间的完整分布及目标附近停留时间，并据此训练策略。框架还可融入机器人任务中的塑形项。

Result: 在七项标准运动与导航基准上，ChronoSRL较对比式、动作分块对比式及生存式强化学习基线学习更快、性能更高，且可使用更小网络。在仿真到现实设置中的四足机器人速度跟踪、目标位置到达和箱体攀爬任务中，它是受测自监督强化学习方法中唯一能够维持指令速度和目标位置的方法，并能攀爬最高的箱体。

Conclusion: 将时间信息显式融入目标表示，并联合建模到达时间与目标附近停留时间，可提升自监督强化学习的效率、可靠性及机器人任务表现。

Abstract: A goal that is close in space can be far away in time. Obstacles, terrain, and the agent's own capabilities determine how long it takes to get there. Yet, critics in contrastive and survival reinforcement learning do not measure the distances in their representation space in units of time. We therefore introduce ChronoSRL, which gives the critic's embeddings an explicit temporal geometry. The distance between state-action and goal embeddings is trained to match the time that the agent takes to reach the goal (goal-reaching time), while goals that were not reached, and goals from other trajectories, are pushed at least one discount horizon away. Furthermore, reaching a goal quickly once does not mean that reaching it is reliable in general, so the policy should not follow the temporal distance directly. Instead, we build on survival reinforcement learning and predict from our temporal embeddings not only the full distribution of goal-reaching times but also the time spent near the goal. Thereby, the policy is trained to favor actions that reach the goal sooner and more reliably and that keep the agent near it. ChronoSRL learns faster and reaches higher performance than contrastive, action-chunked contrastive, and survival reinforcement learning baselines on seven standard locomotion and navigation benchmarks, even with much smaller networks. To test the limits of self-supervised reinforcement learning, we introduce velocity tracking, goal-position reaching, and box climbing tasks with a quadruped robot in a realistic sim-to-real locomotion setup, and show how the shaping terms that are typical for robotics can be naturally incorporated into our framework. ChronoSRL is the only one of the tested self-supervised reinforcement learning methods that learns to stay at the commanded velocities and goal positions, and climbs the highest boxes.

</details>


### [125] [GeoWind2Plan: Mission-Time 3D Urban Wind Prediction for Energy-Efficient UAV Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36056)
*Shaoxiang Qin,Yucheng Zhao,Fuyuan Lyu,Di Zhou,Jiachen Yao,Xue Liu,Anima Anandkumar,Liangzhu Leon Wang,Xiongye Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GeoWind2Plan结合快速三维城市风场预测与无人机路径及速度优化，实现任务时限内的风感知节能规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市建筑会使风场呈现空间变化，影响无人机能耗；但高保真CFD计算耗时长，难以支持通常只有数分钟至数十分钟的任务规划。

Method: 输入背景风向量、三维建筑几何和起终点。框架将建筑几何变换至参考风向坐标系，通过局部几何条件神经算子预测任务相关风场片段，并拼接为可查询的局部风场；随后利用物理约束的无人机能耗模型优化可行的三维路径和速度剖面。以CFD风场评估规划效果，而不要求预测结果完全复现CFD。

Result: 在留出城市区域、风速及任务风向条件下，局部风场推理约需3秒，相比CFD的约8小时大幅缩短。以CFD风场评估时，相较不考虑风的规划，顺风、逆风和侧风任务分别节能6.9%、12.7%和4.5%，分别达到CFD参考节能收益的87.9%、85.7%和75.0%。

Conclusion: 走廊局部化的快速三维城市风场预测能够支持任务时限内的无人机风感知节能规划，并在显著降低计算耗时的同时取得接近CFD参考的节能收益。

Abstract: In urban low-altitude flight, buildings reshape ambient wind into spatially varying 3D flow, making unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) energy depend on local wind exposure as well as path length. However, building-resolved wind information is rarely available when a mission must be planned. Computational fluid dynamics (CFD) can produce high-fidelity urban flow fields, but each simulation is tied to a fixed inflow boundary condition and can take hours to days, which is incompatible with urban UAV missions that typically last minutes to tens of minutes. We present GeoWind2Plan, a geometry-to-wind-to-planning framework for mission-time 3D urban wind prediction and energy-efficient UAV planning. Given only a background wind vector, 3D building geometry, and a start-goal pair, GeoWind2Plan transforms the building geometry into a reference-wind frame, predicts mission-relevant 3D wind patches with a localized geometry-conditioned neural operator, stitches them into a queryable local wind field, and optimizes a feasible 3D path and speed profile using a physically grounded UAV energy model. Rather than pursuing CFD-perfect reconstruction, GeoWind2Plan targets decision-useful wind prediction: trajectories are planned with predicted wind and evaluated under high-fidelity CFD wind. Across held-out urban domains, wind speeds, and mission wind-angle regimes, GeoWind2Plan performs corridor-localized wind inference in about 3 seconds, compared with roughly 8 hours for CFD. Under CFD evaluation, trajectories planned with GeoWind2Plan reduce energy by 6.9%, 12.7%, and 4.5% in tailwind, headwind, and crosswind missions relative to wind-agnostic planning, recovering 87.9%, 85.7%, and 75.0% of CFD-reference savings. These results show that fast, corridor-localized 3D urban wind prediction can make wind-aware UAV energy planning practical at mission time.

</details>


### [126] [Learning from Shared-Control Overrides: Context-Driven Acceleration Profile Prediction for Personalized Overtaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37684)
*Ruizheng Xu,Lounis Adouane,Javier Ibañez-Guzmán,Clément Zinoune*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Context-driven Personalized ACC（CoP-ACC）框架，利用驾驶员在自适应巡航控制中的油门接管行为作为隐式反馈，通过聚类、情境分类和残差回归生成更符合个体预期的加速度轨迹。真实道路数据结果表明，该方法具有较高重构精度，并能减少潜在接管需求、提升乘坐舒适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ACC通常依据平均驾驶员进行标定，在高速超车等时间敏感场景中可能表现得过于保守或不一致，导致驾驶员通过油门覆盖进行干预。本文旨在将这些共享控制接管行为转化为可学习的监督信号，实现面向个体和情境的ACC适配。

Method: 方法采用混合式数据驱动流程：首先对接管事件中的加速度轨迹进行无监督层次聚类，提取具有代表性的驾驶行为模式；随后利用情境分类器，根据操纵前的驾驶条件选择合适的加速度模式；最后通过残差回归器对基础模式进行平滑和个性化修正，以生成适应即时情境的加速度轨迹。该设计避免端到端回归造成的动态响应过度平滑。

Result: 基于真实公共道路数据，并与未参与训练的强制ACC基线进行比较，CoP-ACC表现出较高的轨迹重构保真度，生成的加速度曲线总体趋近于驾驶员在潜在接管场景中的预期行为。摘要未提供具体数值、统计显著性或跨驾驶员泛化指标。

Conclusion: 驾驶员接管行为可以作为有效的隐式监督信号，用于学习个性化、情境感知的ACC策略。CoP-ACC有望提前响应驾驶员偏好，减少人工干预并改善舒适性；但其实际部署仍需进一步验证安全性、长期适应能力、不同驾驶员和复杂交通场景下的泛化性能。

Abstract: Adaptive Cruise Control (ACC) systems are typically calibrated for an average driver, often resulting in a mismatch between vehicle behavior and individual expectations during time-critical maneuvers such as highway overtaking. When the ACC is perceived as too conservative and inconsistent, drivers intervene through throttle overrides, providing implicit feedback on the system's behavior. This paper reframes these override actions as human-in-theloop supervisory signals and proposes a data-driven framework for personalized vehicle adaptation, termed Context-driven Personalized ACC (CoP-ACC). Rather than relying solely on end-to-end regression, which tends to over-smooth dynamic responses, we introduce a hybrid pipeline combining: (i) unsupervised hierarchical clustering to extract representative acceleration profiles from override events; (ii) a context classifier that maps pre-maneuver driving conditions to the appropriate profile; and (iii) a residual regressor that refines the selected profile into a smooth, personalized acceleration profile tailored to the immediate context. Evaluated on real-world public-road data against a withheld forced-ACC baseline, the approach demonstrates high reconstruction fidelity and generates acceleration profiles that tend toward the driver's expected behavior in potential override contexts. The results highlight the potential of learning from shared-control overrides to enable anticipatory, personalized ACC behavior, reducing manual interventions and improving ride comfort.

</details>


### [127] [Brain-SAD: A Brain-Inspired Safe Autonomous Driving Control Framework with Dynamic Fear-Oriented Constraint on Dual-Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38016)
*Huan Rong,Chao Yin,Anouar Imel,Yijie Xia,Tinghuai Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Brain-SAD，一种面向自动驾驶的脑启发式安全强化学习框架，通过动态恐惧约束适应不同交互场景，并在常规驾驶与紧急碰撞防御之间进行策略切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束强化学习方法通常采用静态状态—成本映射或固定安全投影边界，使约束与训练场景强耦合，难以应对复杂且动态变化的交通交互，导致动作代价估计不准确和安全边界适应性不足。

Method: Brain-SAD根据当前车辆交互场景感知并生成动态恐惧信号，在线决定采用面向常规交互的长期策略或面向紧急碰撞防御的短期策略。该信号被构造成两类动态约束：与动作影响直接关联的整体恐惧成本，以及由不同风险邻车推导出的动态可行域边界，并用于在线策略优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，Brain-SAD相较现有方法具有更高的任务成功率、更短的任务完成时间和碰撞恢复时间，并且在复杂度波动的连续交叉路口场景中表现出更强的可靠性。摘要未提供具体数据、基准数量或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 动态恐惧约束能够缓解静态约束强化学习对固定训练场景的依赖，使安全策略更适应实时交通交互。Brain-SAD在安全性、任务效率和跨场景鲁棒性方面展现出潜力，但其有效性仍需通过更详细的消融实验、泛化测试及安全约束违反率等指标进一步验证。

Abstract: Constrained Reinforcement Learning has recently gained increasing attention in the field of Safe Autonomous Driving, where the general mechanism is to maximize the expected reward while keeping the overall action risk bounded. In this way, the safety issues arising in AD can be mitigated through constrained actions. However, existing Constrained RL methods still lack dynamics on the imposed constraints. For instance, the action cost adopted by the existing Primal-Dual/soft-constrained methods is often defined as static state-to-cost mapping, and the safe-action projection in hard-constrained methods relies on the static projection with the fixed feasible region boundary estimated from offline demonstrations. The above drawback tightly couples the imposed constraints to the training scenarios, leaving the AD policy hard to handle different interaction scenarios, due to the improper state-level action-cost and the static projection boundary. Consequently, in this paper, we propose Brain-SAD, a brain-inspired safe autonomous driving control framework with dynamic fear-oriented constraints. By perceiving the current vehicle-interaction scene, Brain-SAD generates dynamic fear signal as fear reaction to online decide long-term policy for regular interaction or short-term policy for urgent-collision defense. In such two policy, the above fear-reaction will be constructed as the dynamic fear constraints, respectively reflecting the overall fear cost directly coupled with action-impact, and the dynamic fear boundary of the feasible region derived from different risky neighbors, both of which will in turn serve for the online policy optimization. Experimental results show that Brain-SAD outperforms existing methods, achieving higher success rate in shorter task-completion and collision-recovery time, and exhibits stronger reliability across continuous intersections of fluctuating complexity.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Multi-Agent Flow Matching with Decoupled Generative Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38133)
*Ruoyu Lin,Magnus Egerstedt,Fabio Pasqualetti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DeGG-Flow，一种面向多智能体流匹配的解耦生成引导框架，可在生成过程中满足硬约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型虽能表示复杂多模态分布，却通常缺乏满足硬性要求的形式化保证；多智能体场景中，约束可能涉及多个智能体，而各智能体又需独立计算自身引导信号。

Method: 将生成过程建模为控制仿射动力系统，针对依赖多个智能体的共享约束，以及依赖邻居的个体约束，构造解耦引导条件，并推导可行性条件、有限时域收敛保证及引导造成分布偏差的 Wasserstein 界。

Result: 在通过环境重构协作跨越空间缺口的多机器人任务，以及满足可供性要求的多物体场景生成任务中，方法均能直接生成满足相应硬约束的结果，并可扩展到训练时未见的团队规模。

Conclusion: DeGG-Flow 为多智能体生成提供了兼顾约束满足、分布控制与可扩展性的统一框架。

Abstract: Generative modeling is widely used for producing diverse objects from complex, multimodal distributions. However, its expressivity does not, in general, come with formal guarantees that the generated objects satisfy hard constraints or requirements. In multi-agent generation, this problem becomes more challenging because a hard requirement can depend on multiple agents, while each agent may need to determine its own guidance input without relying on the simultaneously computed guidance inputs of other agents. To this end, we introduce DeGG-Flow, a general framework for multi-agent flow matching with decoupled generative guidance. By representing the generative process as a control-affine dynamical system, we develop guidance conditions for two classes of coupled requirements: shared requirements whose satisfaction depends on multiple agents together, and private requirements associated with each individual agent dependent on its neighbors. For both classes, we establish feasibility conditions and finite-horizon convergence guarantees. We further derive a Wasserstein bound that characterizes the distributional deviation induced by the guidance. We demonstrate DeGG-Flow on multi-robot collaboration for crossing a spatial gap by reconfiguring the environment, and on multi-object scene generation with affordance requirements. Across both applications, DeGG-Flow directly generates objects that satisfy all corresponding hard requirements, including at team sizes unseen during training.

</details>


### [129] [Graph-Conditioned On-Policy Agent Distillation from Off-the-Shelf Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37522)
*Xiaohan Yi,Wen Luo,Yani Huang,Junfeng Zhan,Asher Qin,Peilin Zhao,Xi Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GC-OPD利用教师执行历史构建状态图，为学生生成的多轮轨迹提供更有执行依据的反馈，显著提升多个任务上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮任务中的错误会逐步累积，使学生偏离教师能够有效监督的范围。仅依赖常规教师反馈，难以为这些轨迹提供充分指导。

Method: GC-OPD将教师多次执行记录按共享状态组织成图，并保留成功与失败历史。每轮学生任务结束后，系统检索当前状态的参考信息或历史替代路径，结合学生的事后反思，对原始思维与动作词元进行评分；无需针对具体任务优化教师。

Result: 相较于标准OPD，GC-OPD在ScienceWorld（4B学生）、ALFWorld Unseen和WebShop上的平均成功率分别由24.70%升至48.78%、由53.36%升至85.26%、由29.10%升至37.65%。在匹配学生规模的比较中，它在ScienceWorld和ALFWorld上也优于所有受测的、使用GRPO训练教师的OPD基线；ScienceWorld上最强的此类4B基线为46.66%。

Conclusion: 将教师执行证据结构化并用于轨迹评分，可增强标准OPD对多轮任务的监督能力，并在多个基准上带来明显收益，且不需要任务专用的教师优化。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains compact language agents with teacher feedback on student-generated trajectories. In multi-turn tasks, compounding errors can move students beyond the teacher's effective supervision. We introduce Graph-Conditioned On-Policy Agent Distillation (GC-OPD), which enriches an off-the-shelf teacher's scoring context with execution evidence. A graph indexes repeated teacher executions by shared states while preserving complete successful and failed histories. After each student episode, GC-OPD retrieves current-state references or historical alternatives and combines them with student hindsight to score the original thought-action tokens. Using the same original teachers, GC-OPD improves mean success over vanilla OPD from 24.70% to 48.78% on ScienceWorld (4B student), from 53.36% to 85.26% on ALFWorld Unseen, and from 29.10% to 37.65% on WebShop. At matched student sizes, it also achieves higher mean success than every evaluated OPD baseline using GRPO-trained teachers on ScienceWorld and ALFWorld; the strongest such ScienceWorld 4B baseline reaches 46.66%. GC-OPD requires no task-specific teacher optimization.

</details>


### [130] [Looped Actor: Depth-Recurrent Reasoning Models for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37432)
*T. Konstantin Rusch,Tim Seyde,Jared Boyer,Zach J. Patterson,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Looped Actor，通过重复调用共享计算模块，让强化学习策略根据当前状态动态分配推理计算；在多项任务中，其表现可媲美或超过参数量大 16 倍的非共享基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定计算预算无法适应不同状态的决策难度。论文从理论上指出，某些马尔可夫决策过程存在状态自适应策略，能够以渐近更少的期望计算达到最优回报；因此，动态循环可能提升序列决策效率。

Method: 构建基于 Transformer 的 Looped Actor：共享计算模块反复更新潜在表示，使其逐步趋近固定点，并依据当前状态决定循环次数。模型在 6 种环境的 22 项任务上接受评估，涵盖组合谜题、机器人操作，以及离线与在线强化学习中的离散和连续动作。

Result: Looped Actor 在整体上达到或超过参数量大 16 倍的非共享基线，优势在需要多步规划的环境中尤为明显。在 Boxoban 中，循环次数会随剩余推箱次数增加；随着循环推进，潜在状态对未来最优推箱动作的预测也逐渐增强。

Conclusion: 共享参数的循环策略为强化学习提供了一种简单高效的自适应计算机制，并有望增强规划能力。结果表明，策略能够将更多计算用于决策难度较高的状态。

Abstract: Looped reasoning models repeatedly apply a shared set of parameters, enabling more computation without increasing the model size. These models also support input-dependent computation by dynamically deciding when to stop looping. Motivated by the recent success of looped transformers in language modeling and reasoning, we investigate whether dynamic looping can similarly benefit sequential decision-making. We provide a complexity-theoretic motivation for this approach by showing that there exist Markov decision processes in which a state-adaptive policy achieves the optimal return with asymptotically less expected computation than any optimal fixed-runtime policy. To learn compute-adaptive policies in practice, we introduce Looped Actor, a transformer-based policy that repeatedly refines a latent representation toward a fixed point using a shared computational block. This allows the model to allocate computation adaptively by varying the number of loops based on the current state. We evaluate Looped Actor on 22 tasks across six environments, ranging from combinatorial puzzles to robotic manipulation and spanning online and offline reinforcement learning (RL) with discrete and continuous actions. Looped Actor matches or exceeds the performance of an untied baseline with 16$\times$ more parameters, with the largest gains in environments where action selection requires substantial multistep planning. For the Boxoban environment, we find that the computation allocation is structured: the number of loops increases with the number of remaining pushes and future optimal pushes become increasingly predictable from the latent state over successive loops. Together, these results highlight actor looping as a simple and efficient way to equip RL agents with adaptive computation and improve their planning capabilities. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [131] [Safe-by-Design Learning via Energy-based Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36942)
*Simone Betteti,Morteza Lahijanian,Luca Laurenti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于能量型现代 Hopfield 网络与端口哈密顿神经常微分方程的安全建模架构，可在设计阶段构造安全屏障函数，并为非线性动力系统提供可认证的不变集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么依赖计算开销较大的事后验证，要么使用缺乏形式化正确性保证的安全机制，难以同时满足复杂动力学建模能力与安全要求。

Method: 将现代 Hopfield 网络整合进端口哈密顿神经常微分方程架构，使模型能够构造屏障函数，并显式确定可接受输入集合及定量鲁棒性半径。

Result: 在多个基准任务（包括一个 12 维纳米无人机模型）上，该方法取得了先进的性能，并生成经认证的不变集；与可比方法相比，这些集合对外部扰动具有更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该架构将安全保证融入神经动力学模型设计，在保持表达复杂非线性动力学能力的同时，实现可认证的安全不变性与鲁棒性评估。

Abstract: Learning neural-network models of dynamical systems with safety guarantees is a fundamental requirement for their deployment in safety-critical settings. Safety is commonly established by proving the invariance of a desired subset in state-space, ensuring that every trajectory initialized in this subset remains confined to it for all time under admissible inputs. Existing frameworks, however, either rely on computationally expensive post-hoc verification or employ safety-enforcing mechanisms without formal correctness guarantees. In this paper, we introduce a novel neural architecture grounded in energy-based modern Hopfield networks to guarantee safety-by-design while retaining sufficient expressiveness to model complex nonlinear dynamics. Specifically, we integrate modern Hopfield networks with a port-Hamiltonian neural ODE, enabling by design the construction of barrier functions yielding explicit admissible-input sets and quantitative robustness radii. Across several benchmarks, including an 12-dimensional nanodrone model, our framework achieves state-of-the-art performance while producing certified invariant sets that are more robust to external solicitations than comparable existing approaches.

</details>


### [132] [RolloutFaith: Auditing Persistent Internal Interventions in Visual World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36843)
*Junchi Yao,Ziyi Wang,Youling Huang,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RolloutFaith，用于评估世界模型内部干预能否改善当前预测并持续改善后续自主预测；实验发现现有拟合编辑器的长期效果有限且不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型会将自身预测作为后续预测的输入，因此只改善干预时刻的输出并不足够。需要衡量干预停止后，改进能否沿模型的后续推演持续保留。

Method: RolloutFaith 在事件、动作、噪声和信息预算固定的条件下，评估干预对当前及后续预测语义质量的影响。研究在 Crafter、Cartpole 和 CoinRun 上测试三个世界模型与十种拟合编辑器，并以真实观测对应的激活替换模型激活，作为参考干预。研究还通过逐项恢复状态组件，追踪干预效果的传递路径；最后提出 Delayed LoReFT，通过四个冻结的未来转移优化低秩干预。

Result: 参考激活替换在全部九种模型与任务组合中改善了后续预测，并在其中八种组合中优于最佳拟合编辑器；但其持续增益在九种组合中的五种会随预测跨度增加而下降。现有拟合编辑器的长期效果有限且不一致。干预效果分别通过 DIAMOND 最新生成帧、DreamerV3 循环记忆，以及 STORM 的两者传递。Delayed LoReFT 在一定程度上提升了干预效果的持续性。

Conclusion: 评估世界模型编辑时，应关注干预对未来自主推演的影响，而不应只考察即时输出。结果表明，提升长期干预效果可能需要在训练目标中纳入未来后果；Delayed LoReFT 为此提供了初步方法。

Abstract: Interpretability methods such as probes, activation patches and learned editors are designed to reveal or modify a model's current computation. World models pose a harder requirement: because their predictions become inputs to later predictions, a useful internal correction must survive after editing stops. We therefore propose RolloutFaith, a framework that measures semantic improvement both in the prediction produced at intervention time and over later autonomous predictions under fixed events, actions, noise, and information budgets. We evaluate ten fitted editors on three world models across Crafter, Cartpole, and CoinRun. We also use Reference Activation Patching, which replaces a model activation with the paired activation computed from the real observation, to measure the correction available at the chosen interface. This reference intervention improves later predictions in all nine model and task combinations and outperforms the best fitted editor in eight, yet its sustained gain decreases with horizon in five of nine combinations. Current fitted editors recover only limited and inconsistent long term effects. By restoring individual state components to their untouched values, we find that persistent effects travel through the newest generated frame in DIAMOND, recurrent memory in DreamerV3, and both in STORM. These findings suggest that training should reward future consequences. To test this hypothesis, we propose Delayed LoReFT, which optimizes the same low rank intervention through four frozen future transitions and improves sustained intervention effects to some extent.

</details>


### [133] [Diffusion Policy Improvement with Proposal-Conditioned Refinement Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36812)
*Junhyun Ha,Juho Lee,Byungwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Proposal-Conditioned Refinement Flows（PReFlow），将基于评论家价值的候选动作筛选与条件流式动作精炼结合，用于离线强化学习中的策略提取。该方法能够表达同一候选动作对应的多个高价值动作模态，并在50个OGBench任务上取得有竞争力的离线表现，在线微调500K步后达到91%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散策略或流策略通过惩罚与行为策略的KL散度来约束策略，可能抑制行为策略密度低但评论家价值高的动作。直接编辑行为策略虽然能够缓解这一问题，但高斯编辑器或确定性编辑器难以表达多个彼此分离的动作模态，因此需要兼顾高价值动作探索、分布约束和多模态表达能力的方法。

Method: PReFlow首先利用评论家函数进行候选动作选择，再通过条件精炼流生成最终动作。作者构造了KL正则化目标，其最优策略表现为在高斯平滑行为先验下的Gibbs策略。精炼流负责表示多个高价值模态，高斯参考分布则约束动作相对于候选动作的过大偏移。基于该高斯参考，方法从采样终点和评论家梯度构造无需仿真的闭式伴随匹配目标，最终只需优化单一的速度回归损失，无需反向伴随求解。

Result: 在50个OGBench任务上的实验表明，PReFlow取得了具有竞争力的离线强化学习性能；经过在线微调后，其综合得分在对比方法中最高，并在500K个环境交互步后达到91%。摘要未报告具体基线、统计显著性或各任务的性能分布。

Conclusion: PReFlow通过候选动作选择与条件流精炼的联合建模，缓解了KL约束导致的高价值低密度动作受抑制问题，并提升了策略对多模态动作分布的表达能力。其闭式伴随匹配目标降低了训练复杂度，实验结果支持该方法作为离线策略提取及后续在线微调的有效方案。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow policies can model complex behaviors in offline reinforcement learning (RL). However, penalizing their KL divergence from the behavior policy can discourage actions having high critic values with low behavior density. Directly refining behavior proposals may be an alternative, yet Gaussian or deterministic editors limit expressiveness to represent multiple separated modes for the same proposal. In this work, we introduce Proposal-Conditioned Refinement Flows (PReFlow), a policy extraction method combining critic-based proposal selection with a conditional refinement flow. To optimize proposal selection and refinement together, we formulate a KL-regularized objective whose optimum induces a Gibbs policy over final actions under a Gaussian-smoothed behavior prior. The refinement flow can represent multiple high value modes, while a proposal-centered Gaussian reference regulates large action changes. This Gaussian reference further enables us to make use of simulation-free, closed form adjoint matching targets from sampled endpoints and critic gradients, yielding a single velocity regression loss without a backward adjoint solve. On 50 OGBench tasks, PReFlow achieves competitive offline performance and the highest aggregate score among the compared methods after online fine-tuning, reaching 91\% after 500K environment steps.

</details>


### [134] [PR-OPD: Privileged Representation On-policy Self-Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36642)
*Muyang Li,Jie Yang,Zhengyu Fang,Junchao Zhu,Zhengkun Xiao,Ruining Deng,Zhe Jiang,Shigang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出PR-OPD，通过对齐带有 hindsight skill 条件下的教师轨迹与无 skill 策略在各层的隐藏表示，增强语言模型智能体的策略学习。在ALFWorld和WebShop上均优于GRPO，最高分别提升4.7和14.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型智能体通常依据每回合单一奖励进行强化学习，特权自蒸馏主要利用 skill 条件下的 token 概率监督。但实验发现，skill 虽显著提升任务表现，却只改变少量采样 token 的概率；相反，它会影响超过80%的响应 token 隐藏状态，并且这些状态变化能够被线性探针追溯到具体 skill。因此，仅进行 token 概率层面的蒸馏可能无法充分利用 skill 信息。

Method: 提出 Privileged Representation On-policy Self-Distillation（PR-OPD）。模型先经过GRPO预热，然后为每条轨迹生成 hindsight skill；接着将该 skill 作为停止梯度的教师条件，使策略重新读取自身响应，并在每一层对齐学生与教师的投影隐藏状态，同时优化原有奖励目标。该方法不需要外部 skill 库或独立教师模型，也不会增加推理阶段开销。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，使用两种不同骨干模型进行评估时，PR-OPD在所有设置中都取得最佳总体结果。相较GRPO，ALFWorld成功率最高提升4.7个百分点，WebShop准确率最高提升14.0个百分点。实验还表明，skill对隐藏表示的影响范围远大于对采样 token 概率的直接影响。

Conclusion: 论文表明，skill信息主要以广泛的隐藏表示变化而非局部 token 概率变化的形式发挥作用。对齐逐层表示能够更充分地传递特权 skill 信息，从而有效提升在线强化学习智能体的性能。PR-OPD是一种无需额外教师、skill库或推理成本的表示级自蒸馏方法，具有较好的通用性与实用价值。

Abstract: Language-model agents are usually trained by reinforcement learning from one reward per episode, and privileged self-distillation enriches it by letting the same policy, given a skill, teach its skill-free self through token probabilities. However, we identify two phenomena that question this channel. Invisible Advantage: a skill in context lifts WebShop success from 42.2% to 56.2%, yet changes the probabilities of fewer than a quarter of the sampled tokens. Much to Align: a skill changes the hidden states of over 80% of response tokens, in a way that linear probes can trace back to the specific skill. To exploit this, we propose Privileged Representation On-policy Self-Distillation (PR-OPD). After a GRPO warm start, the policy writes a hindsight skill for each trajectory, re-reads its own responses with that skill as a stop-gradient teacher, and aligns its projected hidden states to the teacher's at every layer alongside the reward objective, with no external skill library, separate teacher, or inference overhead. On ALFWorld and WebShop with two backbones, PR-OPD achieves the best overall results in every setting, improving over GRPO by up to 4.7 points in ALFWorld success and 14.0 points in WebShop accuracy. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [135] [Act First, Reason Later: Accelerating On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents via Reference-Conditioned Inverse Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36608)
*Zubin Zheng,Jiahao Wu,Shaofeng Zhang,Zhirui Zhang,Yew-Soon Ong,Shengcai Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ActFirst-OPD 将环境交互与完整推理生成解耦，在加速多轮智能体蒸馏的同时基本维持任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 on-policy distillation（OPD）要求智能体在每次短动作前完成较长推理，拖慢环境交互与经验收集；直接生成动作虽更快，却可能降低轨迹质量。

Method: 学生模型依据当前交互上下文和参考的下一观测，通过条件逆动力学推断并执行动作；若状态转移偏离参考轨迹，则切换为自主预测下一动作。收集交互上下文后，模型异步生成完整的“先思考、后行动”响应，用于 token 级教师监督。

Result: 在 0.6B、1.7B 和 4B 参数的 Qwen3 学生模型上，相较 Vanilla OPD，ActFirst-OPD 在 ALFWorld、WebShop 和 ScienceWorld 上的平均训练墙钟时间加速分别为 2.3 倍、1.8 倍和 4.9 倍；在 9 种基准与模型组合中的 8 种，其平均任务成功率达到或超过所有对比 OPD 基线。

Conclusion: 实验表明，多轮智能体蒸馏中，推理不必阻塞动作执行；将交互和完整响应生成解耦，可显著提升训练效率，并在多数测试设置中保持或改善任务成功率。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains multi-turn language agents with dense teacher supervision on student-generated responses. However, standard think-then-act rollouts require lengthy reasoning before each short action, delaying environment transitions and experience collection. Generating actions directly reduces this delay but can degrade rollout quality. To address this, we propose ActFirst-OPD, an act-first, reason-later training framework that decouples environment interaction from full-response generation. The student infers and executes actions through reference-conditioned inverse dynamics using its current interaction context and a reference next observation, and switches to autonomous next-action prediction when the resulting transition deviates from the reference trajectory. From the collected interaction contexts, the student asynchronously generates full think-then-act responses for token-level teacher supervision. Experiments across 0.6B-, 1.7B-, and 4B-parameter Qwen3 students show that ActFirst-OPD achieves average wall-clock training speedups of $2.3\times$ on ALFWorld, $1.8\times$ on WebShop, and $4.9\times$ on ScienceWorld over Vanilla OPD. It matches or exceeds all compared OPD baselines in mean task success rate across eight of nine benchmark-model settings. These results demonstrate that reasoning need not block acting during multi-turn agent distillation.

</details>


### [136] [Action Chunking Proximal Policy Optimization with Feedback Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36250)
*Sanghyun Hahn,Jonghyun Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 ACPPO 及其闭环增强版 ACPPO-Corr，用于提升高维机器人控制中的动作分块强化学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作分块可提供时间抽象，但基于动作块的价值函数难以扩展；开环执行又缺少块内反馈，限制了接触丰富任务中的适应能力。

Method: ACPPO 使用分块策略网络，同时保留标准状态价值评论家，避免学习动作块 Q 函数。ACPPO-Corr 进一步加入逐步反馈校正器，在每个动作块执行期间在线调整规划动作。

Result: 在 IsaacGym 和 Bi-DexHands 的 25 项模拟机器人任务中，ACPPO-Corr 综合表现优于所比较的方法，并在对决策频率敏感和不敏感的任务子集上均表现最佳。消融实验显示，中等动作块长度效果较好，校正器正则化有助于平衡块级规划与局部反馈。

Conclusion: 将动作块规划与闭环在线校正结合，可使动作分块有效应用于在线 PPO；其性能依赖于合适的动作块长度及校正器正则化。

Abstract: Action chunking provides temporal abstraction in reinforcement learning by selecting short action sequences instead of individual actions, but many existing approaches face two limitations in high-dimensional robotic control. First, many rely on value functions over action chunks, which can be difficult to learn as action dimensionality and chunk length grow. Second, executing chunks open-loop removes within-chunk feedback, limiting reactivity in contact-rich tasks. We present Action Chunking PPO (ACPPO), a PPO extension that uses a chunked actor while retaining a standard state-value critic, thereby avoiding chunked Q-functions. We further propose ACPPO-Corr, which augments the chunk planner with a stepwise feedback corrector that adjusts planned actions online within each chunk. Across 25 simulated robotics tasks from IsaacGym and Bi-DexHands, spanning locomotion, arm manipulation, and dexterous hand-object interaction, ACPPO-Corr achieves the strongest aggregate performance among evaluated methods and performs best on both decision-frequency-sensitive and decision-frequency-neutral task subsets. Ablations show that moderate chunk lengths work best and that corrector regularization is important for balancing chunk-level planning with local feedback. These results suggest that action chunking can be effective in online PPO when chunk-level planning is paired with closed-loop correction. The code is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [137] [Dyad: Extending Large Language Models with Native Typed Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36116)
*Yundaichuan Zhan,Weishi Wang,Wenbiao Liu,Daniel Dahlmeier,Chengwei Qin,Juncheng Li,Fredrik D. Johansson,Zhongqi Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dyad，一种将大语言模型与原生类型化决策结合的智能体架构，通过环境条件化动作编码器并行表示候选动作，再与LLM内部状态匹配以生成动作分布，从而提升跨环境泛化与决策效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM智能体通常依赖文本生成或针对特定环境的动作建模，难以高效处理不断扩大的动作空间，也难以学习可复用的状态与动作语义表示。本文旨在实现模块化适配，并增强通用智能体在多种交互任务中的决策能力。

Method: Dyad使用环境条件化动作编码器，将每个候选动作描述编码为向量，并与LLM表示的交互状态进行评分，形成类型化动作分布。该架构将决策分解为交互状态表示和环境特定动作语义表示。作者研究两种强化学习方案：冻结LLM、仅训练动作编码器；以及联合优化LLM和动作编码器，并在多种环境、任务和模型规模上进行评估。

Result: 仅训练动作编码器即可在四个未见环境中稳定获得性能提升，实现无需修改LLM参数的模块化适配。联合训练在多种交互任务和模型规模上优于传统强化学习后训练；在9B模型的ALFWorld任务中，平均绝对性能提升3.80%，同时改善了通用知识、推理和编程能力。

Conclusion: Dyad通过显式建模环境动作语义，为LLM智能体引入了有利于迁移和复用的归纳偏置，并在动作空间扩展时保持较高的评分效率。结果表明，类型化动作决策与模块化动作编码能够有效增强LLM的通用交互能力和强化学习适应性。

Abstract: We study how to build more capable general-purpose agents by extending large language models (LLMs) with native typed decision-making. We introduce Dyad, an architecture that augments a pretrained LLM with an environment-conditioned action encoder that embeds each candidate action description in parallel, then scores these embeddings against the LLM's internal state to yield a distribution over typed actions. By factorizing decision-making into representations of the evolving interaction state and environment-specific action semantics, Dyad introduces an inductive bias for learning reusable representations while keeping action scoring efficient even as the action space grows. We investigate two complementary reinforcement learning settings driven by environment interaction. With the LLM frozen, training the action encoder alone achieves consistent gains across four unseen environments, enabling modular adaptation without modifying any LLM parameters. Jointly optimizing both components outperforms conventional RL post-training across diverse interactive tasks and model scales, including a 3.80% average absolute gain on ALFWorld with a 9B model, while improving general knowledge, reasoning, and coding.

</details>


### [138] [The Decision Value of Perception Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35910)
*Hoang Pham Cong,Ho Viet Duc Luong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 DEEP 基准，用下游决策损失而非感知指标评估额外感知计算的价值；实验表明，升级感知常会损害决策，且计入分配器自身开销后，只有轻量路由器仍优于随机策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 更精确的感知结果不一定带来更好的下游决策，甚至可能使决策变差。因此，感知计算资源分配应依据其对决策损失的实际影响，并与受预算约束的决策预言机比较，而不能把统一高保真推理视为必然最优。

Method: 定义感知计算的决策价值为输入从低成本模式升级到高成本模式后，下游损失的变化；提出 DEEP 基准，在选择、延迟和能耗预算下评估升级前的分配器，并将分配器自身计算开销计入成本。实验使用 KITTI、nuScenes 上部署的单目几何感知，以及在 nuPlan 上以真实检测结果进行开环评估的 PDM-Closed 规划器。

Result: 在会改变下游损失的升级中，34%—54% 会使损失恶化；nuScenes 上，感知层面的收益经常与决策价值方向相反。依据漏检目标带来的感知收益选择可部署信号，相比依据决策价值选择，测试集决策收益平均降低，相当于全低成本模式损失的 7.4%。学习型分配器能找到部分有益升级，但造成的有害升级几乎与随机策略一样多；在 20% 延迟预算并计入自身开销后，只有计算量约为完整检测器一次推理 3.5% 的轻量路由器仍优于随机策略。

Conclusion: 感知升级的价值必须以其对下游决策的影响衡量，单看感知质量可能导致资源错配。DEEP 提供了面向预算约束、计入分配器开销的评估框架；实验也显示，当前学习型分配器仍难以可靠地区分有益与有害升级。

Abstract: Adaptive perception spends extra computation on inputs where perception is expected to improve. When perception feeds a downstream decision system, a better perception output need not produce a better decision. We define the decision value of perception compute as the change in downstream loss from escalating an input from a cheap to an expensive perception mode. Because this value can be negative, the allocation of perception compute should be judged against a budget-constrained decision oracle, with uniform full-fidelity inference as a baseline rather than an upper bound. We introduce DEEP (Decision Evaluation for Escalated Perception), a benchmark that scores pre-escalation allocators against this oracle under selection, latency and energy budgets, charging each allocator for its own computation. With deployed monocular geometry on KITTI and nuScenes, we find that 34--54% of the escalations that change downstream loss make it worse; harmful escalations also occur for the published PDM-Closed planner, evaluated open-loop on nuPlan with real detector outcomes. On nuScenes, perception-level gain frequently disagrees in sign with decision value. This mismatch has practical consequences: choosing among fixed deployable signals by missed-object perception gain rather than by decision value reduces realized test decision gain by 7.4% of the all-cheap loss on average.
