Learned allocators recover part of the oracle's value by finding beneficial escalations but select nearly as much harm as random, and once their own computation is charged at a 20% latency budget, only the lightweight routers, at about 3.5\% of a full detector pass, still beat random.

</details>


<div id='cs.CV'></div>

# cs.CV [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [CoVLM-Bench: A Real-World Benchmark for Cooperative Driving Question Answering and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35823)
*Kang Yang,Shuai Liu,Hang Li,Yance Fang,Deying Li,Yongcai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 CoVLM-Bench 与 CoVLM-Drive，用于在真实车路协同场景中统一评估和研究视觉语言模型的问答与轨迹规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型研究主要关注自车视角；基础设施视角可补充盲区，但传统方法通常先将其转换为几何表示。此前缺少在同一批真实车路协同场景中联合评估问答与轨迹规划的基准。

Method: CoVLM-Bench包含2,196组车路配对帧、35,136条场景问答标注、三部分辅助推理依据，以及基于记录的自车运动生成的未来轨迹目标；规划任务预测未来三秒内的六个航点。标注经模型辅助起草、记录数据计算和人工核验。研究还提出 CoVLM-Drive，直接利用配对视图统一完成问答与规划。

Result: 实验表明，针对合作驾驶问答进行适配可提升回答准确率；CoVLM-Drive的终点位移误差（FDE）低于所比较的V2X规划器。问答任务初始化和推理依据监督均可降低规划误差。

Conclusion: 该基准为车路协同场景中的视觉语言模型提供了联合训练与比较基础；实验结果也显示，问答能力及其辅助监督有助于提升轨迹规划表现。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have made substantial progress in autonomous driving, but their success has primarily been studied in ego-centric scenes. Infrastructure-side observations provide views beyond the ego vehicle's field of view, yet conventional cooperative-driving systems typically transform them into geometric representations for downstream perception and planning. Directly incorporating these views into VLMs offers an opportunity to improve cooperative scene understanding and trajectory planning. However, question answering and trajectory planning have not been jointly evaluated on the same real-world vehicle-infrastructure scenes. We present CoVLM-Bench, a benchmark for cooperative driving question answering (CDQA) and cooperative planning (CP) on vehicle-infrastructure paired scenes. CoVLM-Bench provides scene-grounded CDQA annotations, three-part rationales as auxiliary supervision, and future trajectory targets derived from recorded ego motion. It contains 2,196 paired frames with 35,136 CDQA annotations, while CP predicts six waypoints over a three-second horizon. The annotations combine model-assisted drafting, record-based computation, and human verification. Built upon CoVLM-Bench, we introduce CoVLM-Drive, a unified VLM baseline that directly uses paired views for both CDQA and CP. Experiments show that CDQA adaptation improves answer accuracy and that CoVLM-Drive reaches a lower FDE than the compared V2X planners; QA initialization and rationale supervision each reduce planning error. Together, CoVLM-Bench and CoVLM-Drive support the training and comparison of VLMs for cooperative scene understanding and planning.

</details>


### [140] [Rethinking Representations for World-Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38163)
*Haoyi Jiang,Liu Liu,Xinjiang Wang,Zhihao Sun,Zequn Chen,Sen Wang,Xinjie Wang,Xia Chen,Jingfeng Yao,Weiheng Zhao,Shanglin Yuan,Zhizhong Su,Wei Sui,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReWAM，一种以表征为核心的世界—动作模型，通过预训练DINO特征、特征校准、时序表征瓶颈和动作约束的表征塑形，提升机器人策略学习与动力学建模效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界—动作模型中的表征空间同时服务于控制和未来观测预测，但重建保真度或预训练感知特征单独使用，并不能保证策略学习有效。因此，论文旨在设计更适合世界状态建模和动作决策的紧凑表征。

Method: ReWAM基于预训练DINO特征，使用Feature Calibration对特征进行校准，并通过Temporal Representation Bottleneck构建紧凑的时序世界状态。Action-Grounded Representation Shaping仅将动作损失梯度传递至瓶颈，使策略学习过程决定表征应编码的内容，而世界模型负责学习其动态演化。论文还通过受控对比实验分析不同表征设计。

Result: 在不进行生成式视频预训练的情况下，ReWAM在RoboTwin 2.0上取得93.6%的成功率；在RoboDojo上，使用约600小时具身预训练数据，平均得分为12.29，成功率为8.28%。

Conclusion: 实验表明，面向动作目标塑造并结合时序压缩的表征，比单纯追求重建质量或依赖预训练感知特征更适合机器人世界—动作建模。ReWAM验证了将策略学习与动力学预测在表征层面进行有针对性分工的有效性。

Abstract: World-action models jointly learn robot policies and predict future observations, making the representation space an interface between control and prediction. We study the design of this space through controlled comparisons, finding that neither reconstruction fidelity nor pre-trained perceptual features alone ensure effective policy learning. These findings motivate ReWAM, a representation-centric world-action model built on pre-trained DINO features. Feature Calibration and a Temporal Representation Bottleneck organize these features into compact world states suited to dynamics modeling. Action-Grounded Representation Shaping routes only action-loss gradients to the bottleneck, thereby letting the policy shape what the representation encodes while the world model learns how it evolves. Without generative video pre-training, ReWAM achieves 93.6% success on RoboTwin 2.0. On RoboDojo, it achieves an average score of 12.29 and a success rate of 8.28% using approximately 600 hours of embodied pre-training data.

</details>


### [141] [EVO-WAM: Evolving World Action Models through Video-Action Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38057)
*Shiyang Zhou,Xionghao Wu,Wenbo Li,Shenghe Zheng,Jiyao Zhang,Songsong Yu,Yijun Yang,Jianhui Liu,Haoze Sun,Senqiao Yang,Li Jiang,Jingyong Su,Haoyang Huang,Zhuotao Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EVO-WAM通过筛选并验证模型自身生成的视频—动作轨迹，在不执行候选动作或收集额外专家示范的情况下，提升世界动作模型在新任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型可借助广泛视频先验适应新任务，但生成视频可能未完成任务，且视频与配套动作可能不一致，导致实际执行失败。

Method: 首先加入状态预测和锚定式多帧上下文，使模型能够自主生成完整的自回归轨迹；随后用视觉语言模型筛选完成任务的轨迹前缀，并用逆动力学模型验证视频与动作的一致性；最后迭代训练模型并生成新轨迹。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 的七项未见任务上，Cosmos3 平均成功率由 26.9% 提升至 68.0%，DreamZero 由 28.5% 提升至 46.4%。在真实世界三项未见长时序组合任务上，Cosmos3 平均成功率由 20.0% 提升至 76.7%，增加 56.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究表明，经过任务完成度筛选与视频—动作一致性验证的自生成轨迹，可作为适应新任务的有效训练信号；该方法在模拟与真实场景中均显著提升了任务成功率。

Abstract: Improving robot policies on new tasks without collecting additional expert demonstrations remains a central challenge in robot learning. World action models (WAMs) use broad video priors to jointly predict future videos and actions, offering a potential source of supervision for adapting to new tasks. However, generated videos may fail to depict task completion, and even visually successful videos may be paired with inconsistent actions that lead to execution failure. We propose EVO-WAM, a framework that adapts WAMs to unseen tasks by learning from their own generated video-action trajectories, without executing candidate actions in an external environment. First, we augment WAM training with state prediction and anchored multi-frame context to enable complete autoregressive rollouts without external execution feedback. Second, we identify reliable training experience by selecting task-completing prefixes with a vision-language model and verifying their video-action consistency with an inverse dynamics model. Third, we iteratively train the WAM on verified prefixes and generate new rollouts with the updated model. On seven unseen RoboTwin 2.0 tasks, EVO-WAM increases average success rates from 26.9% to 68.0% for Cosmos3 and from 28.5% to 46.4% for DreamZero, reaching approximately $2.5\times$ and $1.6\times$ their initial success rates. On three unseen long-horizon composite tasks in the real world, it improves Cosmos3's average success rate from 20.0% to 76.7%, a gain of 56.7 percentage points. Project Page: this https URL .

</details>


### [142] [EndoPrior-GS: Dynamic Endoscopic Reconstruction with a Joint Texture Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37874)
*Jiaqi Huang,Shidong Wang,Tong Xin,Kabita Adhikari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EndoPrior-GS通过融合组织纹理先验与时序信息，改善可变形内窥镜场景的3D高斯泼溅重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D高斯泼溅方法用于术中可变形环境时，容易产生伪几何结构，并受光照变化影响。

Method: 从视频帧和估计深度图中构建联合纹理先验，结合器械过滤后的有效组织掩膜、非镜面反射光度筛选及解剖结构显著性，生成概率图以指导高斯基元初始化和密度控制；另加入纹理感知时序项，动态调整训练中成对基元的贡献。

Result: 在EndoNeRF和SCARED基准数据集上，相较代表性方法，Flow Error分别降低27.7%和25.8%，同时保持有竞争力的渲染质量与实时速度。

Conclusion: EndoPrior-GS将视觉启发式先验与深度及时间信息结合，可提升可变形内窥镜场景重建的运动一致性，并兼顾实时渲染。

Abstract: Dynamic endoscopic reconstruction is fundamental to robotic surgery and computer-assisted interventions. While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) realises real-time rendering, its application to deformable intraoperative environments remains constrained by spurious geometry and varying illuminations. To address these limitations, we introduce EndoPrior-GS, a novel pipeline that explicitly couples frame-extracted vision heuristics and estimated depth maps. EndoPrior-GS derives a joint texture prior from a tool-filtered valid tissue mask, a non-specular photometric filter, and anatomical structural salience, yielding a probability map that guides primitive initialisation and subsequent density control. The prior is further extended to the temporal domain through a texture-aware term that dynamically weighs pairwise primitive contributions during training. We conduct extensive experiments on benchmark datasets EndoNeRF and SCARED, and the obtained results show that our method EndoPrior-GS reduces Flow Error by 27.7% and 25.8% over the representative approaches while preserving competitive rendering quality and real-time rendering speed. Our project website is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [143] [Do-JEPA: From Masking to Intervention in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37378)
*Hossein Resani,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Do-JEPA 通过比较同一模拟器状态下两种动作产生的潜在未来，训练模型预测动作造成的变化；实验显示，这有助于提升因果效应预测及其对物理变化的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规潜在世界模型预测后续状态，却难以区分动作导致的变化与仅仅同时出现的变化。已有对象遮蔽方法改变预测器可见的信息；本文改为干预实际发生的模拟动态，以学习动作的因果效应。

Method: 从同一保存状态分别执行目标动作 a 和参考动作 a∅，并以两种结果的潜在表示之差 Δz 为预测目标。Do-JEPA 包含效应损失、动作作用位置的支持损失、效应传播损失，以及约束无关因素保持不变的不变性损失。

Result: 在具有对象对齐变量的合成系统中，支持监督以 99.95% 的准确率定位直接受干预对象；响应起始监督恢复传播图的边 AUROC 为 0.975，高于对照的 0.624。像素实验中，相比使用相同数据训练的对照，潜在效应误差降低 28.4%，自然动作序列上的物理效应误差降低 13.5%。在三个独立生成的 CausalWorld 基准上，物理变化下的响应效应误差约降低 20%，预测效应对潜在环境上下文的敏感度降低 66%。从头训练会损害事实预测准确率，而微调已有模型可消除这一代价。

Conclusion: 对世界动态本身进行干预式训练，能够帮助潜在世界模型更准确地预测动作造成的结果，并提升其面对物理变化时的稳健性；但从头训练存在事实准确率权衡，微调是缓解该问题的一种方式。

Abstract: Latent world models are trained to predict what happens next, so nothing in their objective separates what an action caused from what merely co-occurred with it. Object-masking models such as C-JEPA intervene on what the predictor can see; we intervene on what physically happens. From one saved simulator state we run the dynamics under an action $a$ and under a reference action $a_{\varnothing}$, and train the model to predict the difference $\Delta z=z^{a}-z^{a_{\varnothing}}$ between the two latent futures. The resulting objective, Do-JEPA, has an effect loss, a support loss (where the action enters), a propagation loss (where its effect travels) and invariance losses (what must not change). In a synthetic system with object-aligned variables, support supervision finds the directly intervened object in 99.95% of test cases, where a sparse action mask sends the action to a nuisance slot in every case, and response-onset supervision recovers the ring-shaped propagation graph (edge AUROC 0.975 vs. 0.624). From pixels, the effect loss beats a control trained on exactly the same data: it lowers latent effect error by 28.4% on an end-to-end LeWM model and physical effect error by 13.5% when trained and tested on natural action sequences, and on three independently generated CausalWorld benchmarks it lowers responsive effect error by about 20% under physics shifts and the latent context sensitivity of predicted effects by 66%. Trained from scratch it costs factual accuracy; fine-tuning an existing model with it removes this cost. Together, these results show that intervening on the world, rather than on what the model sees, helps latent world models predict what their actions cause.

</details>


### [144] [UniAfford: Token-Routed Multitask Learning for Generalizable 2D-3D Affordance Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37264)
*Yuhao Liu,Yiming Zhong,Hanqing Wang,Shaocheng Yan,Yuhang Zhang,Wenzhou Lyu,Ziyang Ding,Wei Zhang,Xue Zhao,Jin Pan,Yuexin Ma,Xinge Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniAfford通过任务专属的隐状态路由，将2D与3D可供性识别统一到同一多模态大语言模型框架中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有2D和3D可供性定位在任务定义、监督形式、数据集及评估协议上彼此割裂，限制了跨视觉与几何空间迁移可供性语义的能力。

Method: 提出Token Router for Tasks，将多模态大语言模型的上下文隐状态路由至任务分支，并以分支专属目标直接监督。基于此构建UniAfford及配套数据集，整合像素级2D标注、点级3D标注和语言指令；模型通过模态感知路由器生成图像与点云查询，并分别交由SAM风格像素解码器和基于SONATA的点云解码器处理，支持单模态及联合推理。

Result: 实验显示，该方法在无需针对目标数据微调的情况下，能在2D与3D基准上实现较强的零样本泛化，并在模态隔离评测中取得具有竞争力的分支性能。消融实验支持任务路由、联合2D-3D监督和解码器耦合的作用；语言头诊断表明，路由后的隐状态包含有意义的物体—可供性语义。

Conclusion: 该工作以隐状态任务路由和共享语义表示整合2D与3D可供性感知，为跨模态、跨任务的可供性学习提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Affordance perception aims to localize actionable regions supporting embodied interaction, yet 2D and 3D affordance grounding have evolved as separate problems, with different task definitions, supervision formats, datasets, and evaluation protocols. This fragmentation limits the learning of transferable object-affordance semantics across visual and geometric spaces. We propose Token Router for Tasks, a multitask training paradigm for MLLM-based systems that routes contextual hidden states to task-specific branches without requiring the language head to generate predefined markers. Routed states are supervised directly by branch-specific objectives, enabling dense prediction losses to shape shared MLLM representations. We instantiate this paradigm as UniAfford, a unified framework for generalizable 2D-3D affordance perception, together with UniAfford-Data, a dataset integrating pixel-level 2D annotations, point-level 3D annotations, and language instructions under a shared object-affordance taxonomy, supporting heterogeneous supervision through semantic-level 2D-3D pairing. UniAfford adopts an MLLM as a shared semantic hub and a modality-aware token router to produce image- and point-cloud-affordance queries. These queries respectively condition a SAM-style pixel decoder and a SONATA-based point decoder, enabling flexible 2D, 3D, and joint affordance inference from image-only, point-cloud-only, or paired multimodal inputs. Experiments demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization across 2D and 3D affordance benchmarks without target-specific fine-tuning, alongside state-of-the-art branch-wise performance under modality-isolated protocols. Ablations validate token routing, joint 2D-3D supervision, and decoder coupling, while language-head diagnostics show that routed latent states carry meaningful object-affordance semantics. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [145] [V-JEPA Policy: Building Effective World-Action Models on Predictive Visual Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37250)
*Yang Zhang,Jiangyuan Zhao,Chenyou Fan,Jiayu Hu,Xiu Yuan,Chenjia Bai,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出V-JEPA Policy：利用冻结的V-JEPA 2.1预测视觉潜空间构建世界动作模型（WAM），在不继承完整视觉生成模型的情况下联合学习未来潜状态预测与动作生成，并在多项机器人操作基准上取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM通常依赖大规模预训练的视频生成或图像编辑模型，模型规模大、迁移成本高。本文研究预测性视觉潜空间是否足以作为WAM的基础，并探索无动作标签的视频预训练能否提升下游控制及分布外泛化能力。

Method: 以冻结的0.3B参数V-JEPA 2.1编码器提供视觉潜表示，构建包含指令条件未来潜状态预测器和基于流匹配的动作专家的WAM。二者在单一下游阶段从头联合训练，预测器生成的未来信息上下文键值状态用于条件化动作生成。进一步在DROID视频—指令数据上进行无动作标签的预测器预训练，再将其适配到WAM控制任务。

Result: 总参数量为0.9B，其中0.6B可训练。在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus和RoboCasa-GR1上，模型达到与代表性WAM及视觉语言动作基线相当的性能。在相同下游框架和训练预算下，V-JEPA潜表示优于判别式、重构式及面向视频理解的视觉基础，尤其在分布偏移场景下更明显。利用DROID无动作视频—指令数据预训练预测器，可进一步提升下游控制性能和分布外泛化。

Conclusion: 预测性视觉潜空间能够在不依赖完整预训练视觉生成模型的前提下，为有效WAM学习提供充分基础。未来状态建模知识既可通过任务示范学习，也可从更广泛的真实世界视频中迁移，从而增强机器人控制的泛化能力。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) couple future visual-state prediction with action generation. By adapting video generators or image-editing models pretrained at scale, a prominent line of recent WAMs inherits both predictive knowledge and the models in which it was learned. We ask whether a predictive visual latent space induced by large-scale predictive pretraining can instead provide a sufficient foundation for effective WAM learning without inheriting a complete pretrained visual generative model. To answer this question, we introduce V-JEPA Policy, a simple framework that builds a WAM on the latent space of a frozen V-JEPA 2.1 encoder. An instruction-conditioned future-latent predictor and a flow-matching action expert are jointly learned from scratch in a single downstream stage, with the predictor's future-informed context key--value states conditioning action generation. With 0.9B total parameters, of which 0.6B are trainable, V-JEPA Policy achieves competitive performance with representative WAM and vision-language-action baselines across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboCasa-GR1. Comparing visual foundations under the same downstream framework and training budget identifies V-JEPA latents as more effective than the discriminative, reconstructive, and video-understanding-oriented alternatives, particularly under distribution shifts. Beyond task-specific learning, pretraining the predictor on DROID video--instruction pairs without action labels and adapting it into a WAM yields substantial gains in downstream control and out-of-distribution generalization. Together, these findings establish predictive visual latents as a foundation for effective WAM learning from task-specific demonstrations and for transferring future-modeling knowledge acquired from broader in-the-wild videos. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [146] [InsightMap: Structured Spatial Modeling for Embodied Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37187)
*Hongpei Zheng,Hujun Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InsightMap 将俯视地图用作空间记忆和动作条件预测目标，提升语言导航及多项空间理解任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言导航需要将局部观测关联到持续的空间参照，并理解动作如何改变空间表征；仅依赖观测进行动作预测，难以充分学习这种联系。

Method: 构建 InsightMap，以历史视图与带标签的地图位置建立关联，并使用共享多模态骨干网络联合学习导航动作预测和动作执行后的地图生成。地图预测作为辅助监督，推理时则依据观测到的空间上下文解码动作；另通过对齐的 RGB-D 数据管线支持多种具身空间任务。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 和 RxR-CE 的未见验证集上，成功率分别为 56.9% 和 54.9%。地图预测监督使 R2R-CE 成功率提高 4.3 个百分点、SPL 提高 3.2 个百分点。静态空间任务中，ScanQA CIDEr 为 103.7，SQA3D 精确匹配准确率为 60.1%，ScanRefer 在 0.5 IoU 阈值下的定位准确率为 53.1%。在 Unitree Go2 的走廊、实验室和办公室环境中，表现优于 NaVid 和 NaVILA。

Conclusion: 显式地图记忆与动作条件地图预测能增强导航学习，并支持多类空间理解与定位任务；实验结果表明该框架在仿真基准和真实机器人场景中均具备较强表现。

Abstract: Language-guided navigation requires connecting partial observations to a persistent spatial reference and learning how actions change that representation. We introduce InsightMap, a framework that uses top-down maps as both explicit spatial memory and action-conditioned prediction targets. Historical views are linked to labeled map locations, and a shared multimodal backbone jointly learns navigation action prediction and post-action map generation. Map prediction provides auxiliary training supervision, while navigation inference decodes actions from the observed spatial context. An aligned RGB-D data pipeline supports a common interface for navigation, visual question answering, situated reasoning, and 3D grounding. On the validation-unseen splits of R2R-CE and RxR-CE, InsightMap achieves success rates (SR) of 56.9% and 54.9%, respectively. Adding map-prediction supervision improves R2R-CE SR by 4.3 and success weighted by path length (SPL) by 3.2 percentage points. On static spatial tasks, InsightMap achieves 103.7 CIDEr on ScanQA, 60.1% exact-match accuracy on SQA3D, and 53.1% grounding accuracy at 0.5 IoU on ScanRefer with detected object proposals. On Unitree Go2, it outperforms NaVid and NaVILA in hallway, lab, and office environments.

</details>


### [147] [Multi-Granularity Language-Guided Imitation Learning via Instruction Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37135)
*Yi-Pei Chiu,Wei-Ta Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于指令分解的多粒度语言引导方法，将整体任务描述拆解为具体的子任务级指令，以改进机器人多阶段操作任务的策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言引导策略学习通常用同一整体任务描述指导整段示范轨迹，难以区分多阶段任务中各子任务所需的行为。

Method: 将整体任务描述分解为更细粒度、具体的子任务级语言指令，并在多任务模仿学习设置中评估该方法。

Result: 实验验证了该方法的有效性；摘要指出其能够提升学习效率和任务表现，但未提供具体实验数据或对比结果。

Conclusion: 针对多阶段操作任务，按子任务提供语言指导有望缓解整体指令粒度过粗的问题，并改善策略学习效果。

Abstract: Using language instructions as conditions to guide robot policy learning has recently become an important research domain. However, existing language-guided policy learning methods typically use an overall task description to guide the entire demonstration trajectory. For manipulation tasks involving multiple execution stages, these methods assign the same language description to different subtasks, making it difficult to distinguish the behaviors required at different stages. In this work, we propose a multi-granularity language guidance method based on instruction decomposition. The proposed method decomposes an overall task description into more fine-grained, concrete subtask-level language instructions, thereby enhancing learning efficiency and improving performance. We evaluate the proposed method in the setting of multi-task imitation learning and validate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [148] [Real2Gym: Building Gyms from Videos, Bringing Skills to Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37089)
*Kerui Ren,Yingxiang Xu,Kaiwen Song,Linning Xu,Bo Dai,Mulin Yu,Tao Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Real2Gym 将真实演示转化为可交互的仿真环境，并把仿真中习得的操作技能迁移至真实机器人；实验显示其在仿真和实体执行成功率方面均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实视频包含丰富的操作示范，但要将其转化为可复用的机器人技能，仍需构建视觉对齐的环境、验证可执行的物理交互，并让系统能够从成功与失败中积累经验。

Method: Real2Gym 通过 Real2Sim 重建可编辑场景、对齐物体与相机，并利用原生物理仿真验证示范或重定向动作，同时生成带可行性检查的任务变体。智能体在环境中生成并执行操作代码，依据结果总结成功经验、失败教训、物体相对运动和恢复策略，再通过共享感知与控制接口将技能用于仿真及真实机器人；执行时依据当前观测调整动作，无需更新模型权重。

Result: 评估显示，该方法支持高保真仿真环境重建。在相关仿真环境中，相较 GPT-6 Astra Direct Mode，成功率提高 16.7%，策略执行所需 token 数减少约 74.9%；在 Franka 机器人四项任务的实体执行中，成功率提高 33.3%。

Conclusion: Real2Gym 展示了将真实演示、物理仿真中的经验积累与实体机器人执行整合为闭环流程的可行性，并能在不微调模型权重的情况下复用和适配操作技能。

Abstract: Real-world videos provide rich demonstrations of manipulation, but turning them into reusable robot skills requires visually aligned environments, executable physical interactions, and mechanisms for learning from experience. We introduce Real2Gym, an agentic Real2Sim2Real framework that turns human and robot demonstrations into interactive simulation gyms and brings skills acquired in simulation to physical robots. The Real2Sim module reconstructs editable scenes, aligns objects and cameras with the input, validates demonstrated or retargeted actions through native physics execution, and generates task-conditioned variations with action-feasibility checks. Within these environments, the agent generates executable code for manipulation stages, observes their outcomes, and distills successful attempts and failures into reusable task procedures, object-relative motions, and recovery strategies. Through a shared perception-and-control interface, these skills guide subsequent execution in simulation and on real robots, with motions adapted to current observations and no updates to the underlying model weights. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that Real2Gym enables high-fidelity simulation environment reconstruction, outperforming GPT-6 Astra Direct Mode by 16.7% in success rate with approximately 74.9% fewer policy-execution tokens across these environments, while exceeding it by 33.3% in physical robot execution success rate across four tasks on a real Franka robot.

</details>


### [149] [Speed in the Blind Spot: An Interpretability Analysis of Dynamic Perception in VLMs for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37046)
*Katharina Winter,Stefan Englmeier,Fabian B. Flohr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现，视觉语言模型对速度状态的内部表征、口头输出与时间理解并不一致；驾驶专用训练能增强部分运动表征，但不保证可靠读出或时间 grounding。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型正用于自动驾驶，但其从视觉输入恢复动态物理状态的能力尚未得到充分刻画。研究以速度理解为诊断任务，考察模型对周围车辆速度、自车当前速度和短期未来自车速度的掌握情况。

Method: 在 nuScenes 上评估通用开源模型、PhysicalAI 模型及驾驶专用 Alpamayo-1.5 VLA，采用多种输入与输出形式。分析结合语言输出评测、时间扰动、自车速度反事实提示、隐藏表征线性探测，以及未经专门优化的 QLoRA 任务适配。

Result: 周围车辆速度的个体化表征较弱；自车当前速度通常可从内部表征中解码，但语言输出误差更大：连续探测的 MAE 为 4.7–5.8 km/h，语言输出为 10.2–16.8 km/h。多帧输入带来的语言输出提升不稳定，模型也很少利用帧顺序。QLoRA 可改善任务相关表征和语言读出，但周围车辆连续速度估计仍弱，且多数未来速度提升在打乱帧序后仍存在。Alpamayo-1.5 对周围车辆和未来自车速度的潜在表征较强，但当前自车速度解码能力相近，且仍存在明显的探测—语言输出差距。

Conclusion: 驾驶专用化可以增强部分运动表征，却不能确保场景状态和自车状态都得到更强编码，也不能保证可靠读出或真正利用时间信息。因此，合理的规划输出并不必然意味着模型准确恢复了底层动态状态。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models are increasingly used in autonomous-driving systems, yet their ability to recover dynamic physical state from visual input remains insufficiently characterized. We study velocity understanding as a controlled diagnostic across three tasks: surrounding-agent speed, current ego speed, and short-horizon future ego-speed proposal. On nuScenes, we evaluate open-weight general-purpose and PhysicalAI VLMs, together with the driving-oriented Alpamayo-1.5 Vision-Language-Action model, using multiple input and output formulations. We combine verbal evaluation with temporal perturbations, counterfactual ego-speed hints and linear probes of hidden representations. The tasks exhibit distinct failure modes. Surrounding-agent speed is weakly encoded in an agent-specific form, whereas current ego speed is often internally accessible but poorly verbalized: continuous probes achieve 4.7-5.8 km/h MAE compared with 10.2-16.8 km/h MAE for verbal outputs. Multiple frames provide inconsistent verbal gains to single frame inputs, and frame order is rarely exploited. Under non-optimized QLoRA, task-specific adaptation improves both task-relevant latent speed representations and verbal readout, but continuous surrounding-agent speed estimation remains weak, while most future-speed gains survive frame shuffling, indicating limited temporal grounding. Driving specialized Alpamayo-1.5 shows stronger latent representations for surrounding-agent and future ego speed, while current ego-speed decodability is comparable and substantial probe-verbal gaps remain. Thus, driving specialization can strengthen motion representations but does not guarantee stronger encoding across both scene and ego states or reliable readout. The results show that plausible planning outputs do not necessarily imply reliable recovery or temporal grounding of the underlying dynamic state.

</details>


### [150] [World2Motion: Turning Video World Models into 3D Human Motion Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37004)
*Tu Fangyuan,Xiangyue Zhang,Yiyi Cai,Yichen Peng,Kunhang Li,Bo Zheng,Zhixiang Wang,Kaipeng Zhang,Erwin Wu,Haoran Xie,Haiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: World2Motion将视频世界模型Cosmos 3改造成单阶段的场景感知3D人体运动生成器，可根据单张图像和文本提示同时生成3D运动及对应视频，在保持场景交互能力的同时提升运动—文本对齐，并较两阶段基线实现约3.3倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D运动生成器主要依赖运动数据集，环境覆盖有限，导致场景泛化能力不足；视频世界模型具有更丰富的环境先验，但并非为全身运动生成设计，通常需要先生成视频、再恢复3D运动，推理成本较高。

Method: 方法包含两部分：一是构建融合合成视频—运动对与真实视频—估计3D运动对的训练数据集，以缓解配对视频—运动数据稀缺问题；二是提出解耦偏移噪声调度，在共享去噪进度下为视频和运动分配不同噪声水平，以适应两种模态不同的去噪需求并减少运动抖动，从而将Cosmos 3适配为单阶段3D运动生成器。

Result: 在多源交互基准上的实验表明，World2Motion相较所评估的3D运动生成器具有更好的运动—文本对齐和场景交互能力；其交互成功率达到两阶段基线水平，同时推理速度约提升3.3倍。

Conclusion: 通过多源视频—运动数据和模态解耦噪声调度，World2Motion有效结合了视频世界模型的环境先验与3D运动生成能力，在无需两阶段运动恢复的情况下，实现了高质量、稳定且高效的场景感知人体运动生成。

Abstract: We present World2Motion, a framework that generates scene-aware 3D human motion and corresponding video from a single image and a text prompt. While existing 3D motion generators learn from motion datasets, their generalization is constrained by limited coverage of environments. In contrast, video world models such as Cosmos 3 offer broader environmental priors but are not designed for full-body motion generation; recovering motion from their generated videos requires costly two-stage inference. To address these, we turn Cosmos 3 into a single-stage 3D motion generator. This adaptation has two challenges: the scarcity of paired video--motion data and temporal instability in the generated motion. First, we construct a training dataset combining synthetic video--motion pairs with real videos paired with estimated 3D motion. Second, we propose a shift-decoupled noise schedule that assigns different noise levels to video and motion through shared denoising progress. This design accommodates the different denoising requirements of the two modalities, reducing motion jitter. Experiments on a multi-source interaction benchmark show that World2Motion has better motion--text alignment and scene interaction compared with the evaluated 3D motion generators. It also matches the interaction success rate of the two-stage baseline while achieving approximately 3.3$\times$ faster inference.

</details>


### [151] [SafeVantage: Vantage-Aware Memory for Reliable Embodied Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36906)
*Sean Hardesty Lewis,Zuyi Guo,Benwang Chen,Zirui Liu,Hongyi Lin,Heye Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SafeVantage 将语义记忆与主动视点采集结合，通过保留支持证据的位置与视角，提升部分可观测环境中的判断可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭语义分数难以说明某项判断由哪些视角支持，也无法指出应在哪里获取更多证据；若不区分已确认的正向证据与搜索覆盖范围，决策可能缺乏可靠依据。

Method: 框架为每项主张记录支持视图、相机位姿和目标位置，并利用基于几何信息的可观测性模型预测目标在可达视点是否可见。视点选择依据预期终端决策损失的下降，同时考虑移动成本和几何上不同的交叉验证证据。校准后的判定模块综合支持证据、空间一致性与覆盖情况，输出“是”“否”或“弃答”。

Result: 在覆盖232套未见ProcTHOR房屋的类别存在性基准上，相较于经验证集选择的等动作预算基线，SafeVantage在8次和12次动作预算下的宏平均F1分别提升24.7%和12.0%，且两种预算下风险更低、回答率更高；8次动作时移动距离减少31.7%。等输入HM3D实验显示，在观测固定时其选择性风险更低；受控ScanNet干预表明，恢复支持视图可改善下游视觉语言模型的回答。消融实验也支持可观测性模型对决策质量和采集效率的贡献。

Conclusion: 按主张组织并保留视点级支持证据，有助于连接语义记忆、主动证据采集与可靠决策。实验结果表明，SafeVantage能在提升判断质量的同时减少不必要移动，并更有效地处理证据不足的情况。

Abstract: Reliable embodied decisions under partial observability require informative observations and sufficient supporting evidence. However, semantic scores alone do not reveal which viewpoints justify a claim or where additional evidence should be acquired. We introduce SafeVantage, a vantage-aware semantic memory and active acquisition framework that retains each claim's supporting views, camera poses, and estimated target location, keeping positive support distinct from search coverage. A learned candidate-observability model uses claim-grounded geometry to predict target visibility at reachable viewpoints. These predictions guide view selection through expected reduction in terminal decision loss, accounting for travel cost and geometrically distinct corroboration. A calibrated head then combines support, spatial consistency, and coverage to produce Yes, No, or Abstain decisions. We evaluate SafeVantage on a category-presence benchmark spanning 232 unseen ProcTHOR houses and 7,424 paired episodes per method and action budget. Compared with validation-selected equal-budget baselines, SafeVantage achieves macro-F1 gains of 24.7% and 12.0% at eight and twelve actions, respectively, with lower risk and higher answer rates at both budgets and 31.7% less travel at eight actions. Equal-input HM3D experiments show lower selective risk under fixed observations, while controlled ScanNet interventions show that restoring supporting views improves downstream VLM answers. Ablations further support the contribution of candidate observability to decision quality and acquisition efficiency. Results demonstrate the value of claim-level viewpoint evidence for connecting semantic memory, active acquisition, and reliable decision-making. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [152] [RoXDrive: Closed-Loop Reinforcement Learning for End-to-End Autonomous Driving via Action-Faithful Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36851)
*Hongbin Lin,Chaoda Zheng,Yiming Yang,Xiangyu Li,Shijia Chen,Jinhao Deng,Kangjie Chen,Dongbin Zhang,Jie Feng,Yu Zhang,Xianming Liu,Shuguang Cui,Boyang Wang,Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoXDrive提出一种闭环强化学习框架，通过筛选动作条件与生成视觉动态一致的世界模型轨迹，提升端到端自动驾驶策略的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习主要依赖离线示范，策略无法观察自身动作后果，容易产生因果混淆。现有世界模型方法则面临反事实交互能力有限、仿真与现实差距较大，或生成画面未忠实反映动作条件等问题。

Method: 框架分为两阶段：首先进行模型预训练，并引入动作—视觉忠实度评估器，结合几何感知的辅助轨迹监督，通过逆动力学估计评估长时序生成结果是否符合条件动作；随后进行动作忠实的强化学习后训练，让智能体与世界模型迭代交互，仅保留动作忠实的轨迹，并依据密集安全评分开展场景级闭环策略优化。

Result: 在nuScenes和包含超过13万训练场景的内部数据集上，实验显示该方法在不同规划器上均取得稳定提升；使用DiffusionDrive时，nuScenes上的安全违规减少27.6%，使用Qwen3-VL时，内部数据上的安全违规减少33.7%。

Conclusion: RoXDrive通过识别并筛选动作忠实的世界模型生成轨迹，为闭环强化学习提供了更可靠的训练基础，并在多个数据集和规划器上改善了自动驾驶安全表现。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving policies are commonly trained via imitation learning on logged demonstrations without observing the consequences of their own actions, leading to causal confusion in closed-loop real-world deployment. To address this issue, reinforcement learning (RL) post-training offers a promising alternative by leveraging world models as interactive training environments to enable future scene generation for policy improvement. Nevertheless, existing approaches either rely on reconstruction-based simulators, offering limited counterfactual interaction, or adopt synthetic simulators to enable long-horizon closed-loop interaction at the cost of a substantial sim-to-real gap. Recently, video world models have exhibited the ability to generate realistic multi-step future rollouts but may not faithfully reflect action conditions, resulting in action-vision mismatch. In this paper, we introduce RoXDrive, a plug-and-play closed-loop RL framework that enables reliable policy optimization by identifying action-faithful world-model rollouts, consisting of two stages: 1) Model pre-training: In addition to imitation-based policy pre-training, we devise an Action-Vision Faithfulness Evaluator for inverse dynamics estimation with our geometry-aware auxiliary trajectory supervision, enabling long-horizon assessment of whether visual dynamics faithfully reflect the conditioning ego actions. 2) Action-faithful RL post-training: Agents iteratively interact with world models to form long-horizon scene rollouts, retaining only action-faithful ones for dense safety-aware scoring and scene-level closed-loop RL post-training. Extensive experiments on nuScenes and an in-house dataset with over 130K training scenarios demonstrate consistent gains across planners, reducing safety violations by 27.6% with DiffusionDrive on nuScenes and 33.7% with Qwen3-VL on the internal data.

</details>


### [153] [GlassFormer: Learning Real-time Glass Segmentation using Radar-Depth Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36844)
*Suhani Grover,Astik Srivastava,Viswas Dinesh,Avinash Sharma,K. Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出融合毫米波雷达与 RGB-D 感知的实时透明表面分割框架 GlassFormer，在混合条件和低光环境下均表现出较强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻璃等透明表面常使 RGB 相机看到其后的背景，并导致深度传感器产生无效或背景读数，容易被误判为可通行空间。仅依赖视觉线索的方法在低光、眩光、表面缺乏特征或局部遮挡时性能下降。

Method: 利用毫米波雷达对玻璃表面的强反射，与视觉及深度观测之间的不一致生成雷达引导的空间先验；通过跨模态注意力将该先验融入轻量级 Transformer 分割网络 GlassFormer。

Result: GlassFormer 在混合条件测试集上取得 0.88 mIoU，在专门设计的低光测试集上取得 0.59 mIoU；相较仅使用视觉的方法表现出更强鲁棒性，并可在资源受限平台上实时运行。

Conclusion: 毫米波雷达提供了视觉与深度感知失效时仍可靠的几何线索。将其与 RGB-D 融合，有助于提升透明表面分割在复杂光照条件下的可靠性。

Abstract: Transparent surfaces are ubiquitous in built environments, yet they remain a persistent failure case for robotic perception. RGB cameras perceive the background behind glass rather than the surface itself, while depth sensors such as LiDAR, time-of-flight, and RGB-D often return invalid or background measurements in transparent regions. As a result, systems that rely solely on optical sensing may misinterpret glass walls, doors, or mirrors as free space, compromising safe and reliable navigation. Existing glass segmentation approaches address this by learning visual cues such as reflections, boundaries, and semantic context from RGB images. While effective under favourable lighting and viewing conditions, these cues degrade in low-light environments, under glare, or when glass surfaces are featureless or partially occluded. In this work, we propose a multimodal framework that fuses millimetre-wave radar with RGB-D sensing for real-time transparent surface segmentation. Radar reflects strongly off glass surfaces, providing a geometric cue that remains reliable precisely where vision and depth fail. We exploit this cross-modal inconsistency to generate a radar-guided spatial prior, which is integrated into a lightweight transformer-based segmentation network, GlassFormer, via cross-modal attention. We report results on a mixed-condition test split covering all scene types and a dedicated low-light split designed to stress vision-only methods. GlassFormer achieves 0.88 mIoU on the mixed split, and 0.59 mIoU on the low light split, demonstrating substantial robustness gains over vision-only baselines while maintaining real-time performance on resource-constrained platforms.

</details>


### [154] [Scene Retargeting: Learning Object Placement with Analogical Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36801)
*Minkwan Kim,Junho Kim,Seungmin Lee,Changwoon Choi,Young Min Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出场景重定向框架，将语义连贯的空间组织从一个布局稳定迁移到另一个布局，并兼顾场景特性与物理约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实交互式仿真需要支持日常活动的三维场景，但稀疏且不规则的布局具有场景特定的物理限制，导致难以建立可泛化的功能场景生成方法。

Method: 以对象簇为单位迁移空间组织，适应对象实例不匹配和不同平面布局；通过保留基础特征的空间分布维护簇内语义上下文，再施加墙面接触、对象对齐以及通道和开口畅通等物理约束进行细化。

Result: 在3D-FRONT布局生成任务中，方法优于现有先进方法；并展示了真实场景到仿真、类比轨迹迁移和多参考场景组合等下游应用。

Conclusion: 该方法避免依赖文本描述或成对关系，通过迁移簇级空间组织实现对布局差异和物理条件的适应，为功能性三维场景生成及相关应用提供了通用框架。

Abstract: Interactive simulations of embodied AI or spatial computing applications build on realistic 3D scenes that support daily activities. However, sparse, irregular layout structures impose scene-specific physical constraints, making it hard to define a generalizable framework for generating similar functional context. We formalize Scene Retargeting as stably transferring the semantically coherent spatial organization across layouts, rather than relying on textual descriptions or pairwise relationships. Our cluster-wise transfer flexibly handles mismatched object instances and adapts to distinctive floor plans. We optimize to preserve the rich semantic context of individual clusters by respecting the spatial distribution of foundation features. We can then impose physical constraints to refine wall contacts, pairwise alignment, or clear passageways and openings. Our framework outperforms state-of-the-art methods on layout generation on the 3D-FRONT dataset, and demonstrates downstream applications including real-to-sim transfer, analogical trajectory transfer, and multi-reference composition.

</details>


### [155] [DynamicHOI: Coupled Dynamics for Physics-aware HOI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36454)
*Wenliang Guo,Zhanbo Huang,Yu Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 DynamicHOI，一种结合几何约束与耦合动力学的物理感知手—物体交互重建框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目视频观测不完整，现有方法虽能获得视觉上合理的轨迹，却常忽略交互动力学，导致机械不一致。

Method: 利用几何引导的扩散细化轨迹；通过关节逆动力学与牛顿—欧拉动力学估计手部广义力和物体力旋量；以接触力传递耦合手与物体动力学，并对手部主动驱动力的经验分布建立概率先验，抑制不合理动作。

Result: 在三个手—物体交互数据集上，方法稳定提升了手部和物体重建效果；重建轨迹也有助于手部世界模型生成和机器人操作学习等下游任务。

Conclusion: 将几何证据、手物耦合动力学及主动驱动力先验纳入重建，有助于生成更符合物理规律的交互轨迹，并拓展其下游应用价值。

Abstract: We study hand-object interaction (HOI) reconstruction from monocular RGB videos, where partial observations can produce visually plausible yet mechanically inconsistent trajectories. Existing methods mainly enforce visual and geometric agreement, leaving the underlying interaction dynamics insufficiently constrained. We propose DynamicHOI, a physics-aware HOI reconstruction framework combining geometry-grounded diffusion refinement with coupled hand-object dynamics. Geometry spatially grounds visual evidence for trajectory refinement, while articulated inverse dynamics and Newton-Euler dynamics derive hand generalized forces and object wrenches for dynamics-level supervision. We further couple hand and object dynamics through contact-force transfer and recover active hand actuation as an interaction-level physical quantity. We formulate its empirical magnitude distribution into a probabilistic prior that penalizes unlikely actuation and suppresses mechanically implausible reconstructed motion. Experiments on three HOI datasets show consistent improvements in both hand and object reconstruction. The reconstructed trajectories further benefit downstream applications including hand world-model generation and robotic manipulation learning, demonstrating the value of physics-aware HOI modeling beyond reconstruction.

</details>


### [156] [Temporal-Aware Fusion for Robust Outdoor LiDAR Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36432)
*Minghang Zhu,Zhijing Wang,Yuxin Guo,Chen Liu,Yongshu Huang,Wen Li,Sheng Ao,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TempLoc，一种面向户外LiDAR重定位的时序感知框架，通过建模连续扫描之间的一致性，提高动态或场景歧义条件下的全局6-DoF位姿估计精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于回归的LiDAR重定位方法通常依赖单帧推理，未充分利用连续扫描中的时空一致性，因此在动态环境和外观相似场景中容易出现定位不稳定或误匹配问题。

Method: TempLoc包含三个模块：1）全局坐标估计模块，为每帧LiDAR点预测全局坐标及其不确定性；2）先验坐标生成模块，通过注意力机制估计帧间点对应关系；3）不确定性感知坐标融合模块，根据预测不确定性融合点对应信息与全局坐标预测，端到端生成更稳定、准确的全局6-DoF位姿。

Result: 在NCLT和Oxford RobotCar基准数据集上的实验表明，TempLoc显著优于现有先进方法，验证了时序感知点对应建模和不确定性感知融合的有效性。摘要未提供具体的数值提升、定位误差或召回率。

Conclusion: 通过联合利用单帧全局坐标、帧间点对应关系及预测不确定性，TempLoc能够增强LiDAR重定位的时序一致性和环境适应性，为动态及歧义场景下的鲁棒户外定位提供了有效方案。

Abstract: LiDAR relocalization aims to estimate the global 6-DoF pose of a sensor in the environment. However, existing regression-based approaches often encounter limitations in dynamic or ambiguous scenarios, as they typically prioritize single-frame inference, leaving the potential of spatio-temporal consistency across scans not fully explored. In this paper, we propose a Temporal-aware Localization framework (TempLoc) designed to enhance the robustness of outdoor localization by effectively modeling sequential consistency. Specifically, a Global Coordinate Estimation module is first introduced to predict point-wise global coordinates and associated uncertainties for each LiDAR scan. A Prior Coordinate Generation module is then presented to estimate inter-frame point correspondences by the attention mechanism. Lastly, an Uncertainty-Guided Coordinate Fusion module is deployed to integrate both predictions of point correspondence in an end-to-end fashion, yielding a more temporally consistent and accurate global 6-DoF pose. Experimental results on the NCLT and Oxford RobotCar benchmarks show that our TempLoc outperforms state-of-the-art methods by a large margin, demonstrating the effectiveness of temporal-aware correspondence modeling in LiDAR relocalization.

</details>


### [157] [StructRL: Online Structured Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Vision-Language-Action Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36352)
*Ziyi Yin,Sangmin Woo,Kang Zhou,Sungyeon Kim,Aosong Feng,Haibo Ding,Jun Huan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StructRL是一种面向视觉-语言-动作模型的在线强化学习框架，通过可验证子任务构建结构化中间奖励，并依据任务完成速度调整奖励，从而提升长时域、多步骤操作任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在短时域操作任务上表现较好，但处理需要多个相互依赖操作的长时域任务时仍较困难。传统在线强化学习通常仅在任务完全成功后提供终止奖励，导致奖励稀疏，且无法区分早期失败与已取得较大部分进展的轨迹。

Method: StructRL首先将完整任务分解为一系列可验证的子任务；仅当其前置子任务完成后，才给予相应的中间奖励，以保持任务依赖关系。同时，根据子任务的完成进度或速度对奖励进行缩放，形成具有结构化和密集性的在线强化学习监督信号。该方法在RoboCasa365和LIBERO-Long上，分别结合GR00T-N1.5与pi 0.5进行评估，并与现有在线强化学习基线比较。

Result: 在RoboCasa365和LIBERO-Long两个基准上，使用GR00T-N1.5和pi 0.5的实验均表明，StructRL稳定优于所评估的在线强化学习基线，说明结构化中间奖励能够有效改善长时域任务学习。摘要未提供具体的性能提升数值。

Conclusion: 可验证的子任务分解、依赖感知的中间奖励以及基于完成速度的奖励缩放，能够缓解终止奖励稀疏问题，提升VLA模型在长时域、多阶段操作任务中的在线强化学习后训练效果。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models perform well on shorter-horizon manipulation tasks but still struggle with long-horizon tasks that require multiple dependent manipulations from a single command. Online reinforcement learning (RL) can improve these policies through environment interaction, yet many existing methods provide reward only after the complete task succeeds. However, such terminal supervision is sparse and does not distinguish early failures from rollouts that make substantial partial progress. We propose StructRL, an online RL framework that constructs structured intermediate supervision from verifiable subtask completions. StructRL decomposes each task into verifiable subtasks, grants intermediate rewards only after the prerequisite subtasks have been completed, and scales each reward according to completion pace. Across RoboCasa365 and LIBERO-Long with GR00T-N1.5 and pi 0.5, StructRL consistently outperforms evaluated online RL baselines. These results show that verifiable, structured intermediate rewards improve long-horizon VLA post-training. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [158] [AerialDojo-200K: A Large-Scale Benchmark Suite for Open-World Aerial Object-Goal Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36066)
*Tongtong Feng,Xin Wang,Haoran Hou,Ren Wang,Weiran Wang,Shaokai Zhu,Ziqi Jia,Hao Wang,Yu-Wei Zhan,Zongyuan Wu,Jinghao Cui,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 AerialDojo-200K，一个面向开放世界空中目标搜索的大规模基准套件，旨在支持可扩展训练与跨场景评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究依赖规模小、环境特定且动作空间和数据格式不统一的基准，限制了空中智能体的训练规模、泛化能力及公平比较。

Method: 构建涵盖 42 个仿真场景的基准，覆盖四类场景家族和 21 种场景类型；人工标注地标、目标物体及其锚点，并生成语义目标与图像目标任务。每项任务均配有无碰撞参考轨迹和多视角视频。另设统一评测框架，将场景划分为 21 个分布内场景和 21 个分布外场景。

Result: 基准包含 205,732 个任务实例，覆盖 Base、Standard 和 Long-Horizon 设置；其场景数是此前最大基准的 3 倍，任务实例数为 18.7 倍。对 5 个开源和 4 个闭源多模态大语言模型的评测显示，它们在该任务上仍面临显著挑战。

Conclusion: AerialDojo-200K 提供了规模更大、格式统一且兼顾分布内外评测的资源，可用于推动开放世界空中目标搜索研究；评测结果表明，通用空中智能体尚未成熟。

Abstract: Open-world aerial object-goal search is a foundational yet challenging task, requiring aerial agents to autonomously explore large-scale, unstructured three-dimensional environments and reach target objects specified by semantic descriptions or reference images, rather than following route-specific instructions. However, research in this task remains at a nascent stage and relies on small, environment-specific benchmarks with heterogeneous action spaces and data formats. These limitations hinder large-scale training and cross-benchmark evaluation, constraining the scalability and generalizability of aerial agents. To address this problem, we propose AerialDojo-200K, a large-scale benchmark suite for open-world aerial object-goal search, with 3 times as many scenes and 18.7 times as many task instances as the largest existing benchmark for this task. Specifically, we construct 42 simulation scenes spanning four scene families and 21 scene types, including 18 urban, 12 natural, six infrastructure, and six disaster scenes. To ensure data quality, 12 annotators spent two months manually annotating 109 landmarks, 2099 target objects, and 2099 object anchors across these scenes. We further construct 205,732 task instances, comprising over 100K semantic-goal and over 100K image-goal instances across Base, Standard, and Long-Horizon settings. Each task instance includes a collision-free reference trajectory and corresponding multi-view video recordings. We also develop a unified evaluation framework with a scene partition comprising 21 in-distribution scenes and 21 out-of-distribution scenes. Finally, our evaluation of five open-source and four closed-source multimodal large language models reveals that there is still a long way to go toward achieving general-purpose aerial agents. All can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [159] [CoDimRecon: Agentic Reconstruction of Sim-Ready 3D Scenes with Deformable Curves, Surfaces, and Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36024)
*Shuzhao Xie,Lelin Wang,Guying Lin,Zhi Wang,Minchen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoDimRecon 从多视角 RGB 图像重建可编辑、可仿真的场景，涵盖刚体、关节物体及不同类型的可变形物体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景重建方法主要针对刚体，难以为曲线、薄壳和实体等不同维度的可变形物体生成适用于仿真的几何与物理模型。

Method: 框架结合场景级几何先验与物体级网格生成结果，由智能体重建场景布局和物体几何，并将关节物体拆分为带显式关节的可动部件。针对可变形物体，分别构建带半径的中心线、具有厚度的流形薄壳和用于体网格划分的封闭实体；再利用可复用的仿真技能初始化物理模型，并通过行为测试发现不匹配，定向调整运动、几何、数值设置或材料模型。

Result: 在 Replica 和 ScanNet++ 场景上，CoDimRecon 达到有竞争力的组合式重建精度，并能生成用于杆状物、薄壳和实体仿真的可变形资产。研究还展示了三种表示下的机器人交互，以及通过行为测试识别并采用塑性弯曲模型的纸张折叠案例。

Conclusion: 该方法将场景重建、可编辑几何与物理行为验证结合起来，扩展了仿真就绪重建对可变形物体及其不同表示形式的支持。

Abstract: Reconstructing simulation-ready 3D scenes from real-world observations enables robotics, gaming, and immersive applications, yet existing methods largely assume rigid objects. This leaves an important gap for deformables, whose simulation-ready geometry depends on dimensionality (curves, surfaces, or volumes) and whose behavior may require models beyond elasticity. We present CoDimRecon, an agentic framework that reconstructs editable scenes containing rigid, articulated, and deformable objects from multi-view RGB observations. Scene-level geometric priors ground scale and layout, while object-level generated meshes guide the agent toward detailed, compact geometry; articulated rigid objects are decomposed into movable parts with explicit joints. For deformables, category-wise agent sessions reconstruct curves as centerlines with radii, surfaces as manifold shells with thickness, and volumes as watertight solids for volumetric meshing. Reusable simulator skills initialize compatible physical models and parameters, while agent-guided behavioral tests expose mismatches and trigger targeted revisions of motion, geometry, numerics, or material modeling. On evaluated Replica and ScanNet++ scenes, CoDimRecon achieves competitive compositional reconstruction accuracy while additionally producing deformable assets for rod, shell, and solid simulation. We further demonstrate robot interactions across all three representations, including a controlled paper-folding case in which behavioral testing motivates plastic bending.

</details>


### [160] [Systematic Multi-Agent Vision-and-Language Navigation: Formulation, Benchmark, and Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35965)
*Yunzhe Xu,Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将视觉-语言导航扩展为多智能体协同问题，构建基准数据集 MAVLN，并提出协调导航系统 TRISS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言导航主要研究单个智能体执行单条指令，难以覆盖需要多机器人协作、且受依赖关系与资源限制的现实任务。

Method: 将多智能体导航形式化为受约束的协同问题，任务子项包含依赖约束和资源约束（presence locks 与 holding chains）。通过经验证的四阶段流程构建 MAVLN，并设计适配约束的评测指标。提出 TRISS，整合基于大语言模型的子任务调度器、共享拓扑记忆，以及用于生成无碰撞路线的冲突感知执行机制。

Result: MAVLN 包含 145 个场景中的 11,724 个任务片段，最多支持四名智能体，并涵盖三种指令设置。实验表明，TRISS 可作为综合基线；在调度、规划和执行方面仍有显著提升空间。

Conclusion: 该工作系统化定义并基准化了受任务约束的多智能体视觉-语言导航，同时提供可复现的基线方案，凸显了协调、资源管理与安全执行方面的研究挑战。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) has largely focused on a single agent following a single instruction, yet many real-world applications require teams of robots to tackle tasks beyond the capabilities of any individual agent. We present Systematic Multi-Agent Vision-and-Language Navigation, providing, to our knowledge, the first systematic formalization of multi-agent VLN as a constrained coordination problem: each mission consists of subtasks carrying dependency and resource constraints (presence locks and holding chains). A verified four-stage crafting pipeline instantiates the task as MAVLN, comprising 11,724 episodes across 145 scenes with teams of up to four agents under three instruction regimes, accompanied by tailored constraint-aware metrics. We further present TRISS, a coordination-ready navigation system coupling an LLM-based subtask scheduler, a shared topological memory that turns each agent's exploration into team knowledge, and a conflict-aware execution mechanism that realizes simultaneous intentions as collision-free routes. Extensive experiments establish TRISS as a comprehensive baseline and reveal substantial room for improvement across scheduling, planning, and execution, highlighting the challenges of coordinating under MAVLN task constraints. Project page: this https URL .

</details>
